### [1] [HYPIC: Accelerating Hybrid-Attention LLM Serving with Position-Independent Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01299)
*Yifei Liu,Juntong Wu,Yang Liu,Junhao Hu,Minghao Li,Xiaoxu Chen,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Hypic系统，首次实现了混合注意力大语言模型的位置无关缓存，通过段累积转移算子和边界缝合窗口机制，在保持精度的同时显著降低了延迟并提升了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG和智能体LLM服务中，提示词由独立片段组装成长上下文，导致预填充阶段成为计算瓶颈。现有的两种优化方向——位置无关缓存(PIC)和混合注意力模型——无法共存：将PIC应用于混合注意力模型会失效，因为逐token的KV缓存复用原语无法迁移到逐请求的循环状态。

Method: 针对线性注意力层，识别出段累积转移算子作为缺失的代数原语，将其与每个段的零起始终止状态一起缓存，实现独立缓存段的近似精确且常数时间状态组合。针对剩余的全注意力层，发现最重要的注意力偏差集中在段边界，只需在每个边界重新计算小的缝合窗口即可恢复跨段回溯。此外，利用段级自包含性在实例间并行化缓存未命中的预填充。

Result: 在四个混合注意力模型和五个工作负载上评估，Hypic平均将首token时间(TTFT)降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升高达2.0倍，同时保持在完全重计算精度的3.3个精度点范围内。

Conclusion: Hypic成功解决了混合注意力模型与位置无关缓存的不兼容问题，将长冷请求从主要的尾部延迟贡献者转变为可加速的工作负载，为RAG和智能体LLM服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In retrieval augmented generation (RAG) and agentic LLM serving, prompts are assembled from independent segments into long contexts, making the prefill stage dominate the per-request computation cost. To this cost, two directions have emerged in parallel: position-independent caching (PIC) admits KV reuse for non-contiguous segments shared across different requests, while hybrid-attention models reduce computation complexity by replacing most full-attention layers with linear attention. However, they cannot coexist: applying PIC to hybrid-attention models breaks down because per-token KV-cache reuse primitives do not transfer to the per-request recurrent state.
  In this work, we present Hypic, the first serving system for hybrid-attention LLMs with position-independent caching. For linear-attention layers, we identify the segment-cumulative transition operator as the missing algebraic primitive, and cache it alongside each segment's zero-start end-state, enabling near-exact and constant-time state composition of independently cached segments. For the remaining full-attention layers, existing PIC methods also fail as linear layers do not expose the per-token hidden states for selective recomputation. We show that the most significant attention deviation concentrates at segment boundaries, so recomputing only a small seam window at each boundary suffices to restore cross-segment lookback. Finally, Hypic exploits segment-level self-containment to parallelize cache-miss prefill across instances, turning long cold requests -- a major tail-latency contributor under both prefix caching and prior PIC -- into an accelerable workload. Evaluated across four hybrid-attention models and five workloads, Hypic reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 2.45x on average and improves peak throughput by up to 2.0x over existing systems, while staying within 3.3 points of full-recompute accuracy.

</details>


### [2] [SLFS: a Flexible, Low-Cost Distributed File System Using Serverless Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01486)
*Cheng Hao,Yang,Paola Alsharabaty,Soufiane Jounaid,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Ji-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SLFS是首个基于Serverless架构的分布式文件系统，通过多线程短生命周期服务器设计解决冷启动问题，在保持低延迟的同时降低了高达68%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式文件系统需要为峰值需求配置资源，但文件访问模式波动显著，导致非高峰期大量容量闲置。现有扩展机制以整个服务器为粒度操作，耗时数分钟到数小时，无法追踪文件系统常见的快速、细粒度负载变化。Serverless计算具有毫秒级粒度弹性和按使用付费的特点，提供了引人注目的替代方案。

Method: SLFS在键值存储之上实现文件服务，保持函数操作简单且短暂；引入新颖的多线程、短生命周期服务器设计，在保持低成本的同时克服冷启动问题；采用策略执行的协调器高效地将文件映射到函数实例、弹性扩展系统并控制函数生命周期以平衡性能和成本；可灵活运行在从S3等云原生服务到用户管理的键值存储等不同存储后端上。

Result: SLFS将冷启动问题缓解了580倍（相比基础Serverless设计）；成本分别比λFS、EFS和Ceph降低了高达63%、68%和63%。

Conclusion: SLFS证明了基于Serverless构建分布式文件系统的可行性，通过创新的设计有效解决了冷启动问题，实现了显著的性能和成本优势，同时提供了可配置的成本-性能权衡能力。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed file systems must provision resources for peak demand, yet file access patterns fluctuate significantly, leaving substantial capacity idle during off-peak periods. Existing scaling mechanisms operate at the granularity of entire servers and take minutes to hours, making them unable to track the rapid, fine-grained load variations that file systems commonly experience. Serverless computing, with its millisecond-granularity elasticity and pay-per-use pricing, offers a compelling alternative. We present SLFS, the first distributed file system built with serverless functions for both data and metadata operations. SLFS implements file services on top of key-value stores, keeping function operations simple and short, and introduces a novel multi-threaded, short-lived server design that overcomes the cold-start problem while maintaining low cost. A policy-enforcing coordinator efficiently maps files to function instances, scales the system elastically, and controls function lifetimes to balance performance and cost. SLFS can flexibly run on diverse storage backends -- from cloud-native services like S3 to user-managed key-value stores -- enabling configurable cost-performance trade-offs. Our evaluation shows that SLFS mitigates cold starts by 580$\times$ compared to the base serverless design and outperforms $λ$FS, EFS, and Ceph at up to 63%, 68%, and 63% lower cost, respectively.

</details>


### [3] [OmniPilot: An Uncertainty-Aware LLM Inference Advisor for Heterogeneous GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01579)
*D. Balamurugan,Thomas W. Bush*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了OmniPilot，一个面向共享异构GPU集群的LLM推理启动顾问系统，通过保形校准的分位数成本模型和分布外拒绝层，在八个服务目标上实现配置预测与排序，在支持范围内达到6.2% MAPE和95% top-1准确率，并能成功识别分布外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享异构GPU集群上部署大语言模型时，用户和运维人员需要在消耗宝贵节点时间之前选择GPU类型、张量并行度和精度。这一决策具有挑战性：有效吞吐量、启动成功率和集群需求利用率持续波动；静态配置方案忽略了关键交互作用（量化效果依赖模型家族、KV缓存压力造成尺寸-精度权衡、不同张量并行度的失败率差异超过两倍）；集群资源常受不可预测的硬件故障限制。

Method: 提出OmniPilot启动顾问系统，采用：1）保形校准的分位数成本模型，覆盖八个服务目标；2）分布外（OOD）拒绝层，当请求超出测量支持范围时拒绝预测；3）基于经济效用指标的配置排序方法，该校准于运维人员的显示偏好。系统通过预测可行配置的服务成本来指导决策。

Result: 在A100、H100和H200硬件上、四种精度下的460个基准测试中，OmniPilot实现：聚合吞吐量预测MAPE为6.2%，对数空间R²=0.92；top-1准确率达95%，平均效用遗憾仅0.003。在分布外留出测试集（不支持单元）上，预测误差升至24-46%，保形区间覆盖率为0/5；但拒绝层成功将所有五个样本标记为低置信度。

Conclusion: OmniPilot成功解决了共享异构GPU集群上LLM推理配置选择的动态性和复杂性挑战。通过保形校准和OOD拒绝机制，系统在支持范围内提供高精度预测，并能可靠地识别并拒绝超出支持范围的请求。随着时间推移，这些OOD场景将被整合到训练数据集中，以持续扩展顾问系统的支持范围。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) on a shared, heterogeneous GPU cluster requires users and operators to select the GPU type, tensor-parallel degree, and precision before committing valuable node-hours. Making these choices is challenging because effective throughput, launch-success rates, and cluster demand and utilization continuously fluctuate. Furthermore, static configuration recipes miss critical interactions: quantization effects depend heavily on the model family, key-value cache pressure creates size-by-precision trade-offs, and failure rates vary by more than twofold across different tensor-parallel degrees. Additionally, cluster resources are frequently constrained by unpredictable hardware failures. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{OmniPilot}, a launch advisor that predicts serving costs for feasible configurations and abstains when requests fall outside its measured support envelope. OmniPilot pairs a conformally calibrated quantile cost model (spanning eight serving targets) with an out-of-distribution (OOD) abstention layer. It ranks configurations using an economic utility metric calibrated to an operator's revealed preferences. In evaluations across 460 benchmark runs on A100, H100, and H200 hardware across four precisions, OmniPilot predicts aggregate throughput with a 6.2\% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) and a log-space $R^2=0.92$. The advisor achieves 95\% top-1 accuracy with a mean utility regret of just 0.003. When tested on an OOD holdout of unsupported cells, prediction error climbs to 24-46\% and conformal intervals cover 0 of 5 points; however, the abstention layer successfully flags all five as low-confidence. Over time, these OOD scenarios will be integrated into the training dataset to continuously expand the advisor's support envelope.

</details>


### [4] [Arachne: Orchestrating Cascades for Efficient Text-to-Video Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01701)
*Peng Yu,Yuankai Fan,Yang Qiu,Tian Li,Bihuan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Arachne，一个用于大规模文本到视频（T2V）模型训练的高效框架。通过将训练过程分解为细粒度的计算单元（cascades），并协调其分布式执行与同步，Arachne在迭代时间上相比现有框架减少了高达65%，且随着训练规模的扩大，性能优势更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模文本到视频（T2V）模型需求的增长，训练数据在分辨率和时长上呈现高度异质性。现有的bucketing策略在静态并行方案下难以随计算和数据规模扩展，导致显著的工作负载不平衡和硬件利用率不足。因此，亟需一种能够高效处理数据异质性并能随规模扩展的训练框架。

Method: 提出Arachne训练框架，将训练过程分解为细粒度的计算单元，称为cascades。通过协调的空间和时间优化，实现这些cascades在集群中的分布式执行和同步，从而提升训练效率和资源利用率。

Result: 综合评估显示，Arachne相比主流框架在迭代时间上最多减少了65%，并展现出正向的扩展趋势：随着训练规模的增加，其性能优势进一步增强。

Conclusion: Arachne通过细粒度分解和协调优化，有效解决了大规模T2V模型训练中的数据异质性和负载不平衡问题，实现了显著的性能提升和良好的扩展性，适合于更大规模的训练场景。

Abstract: The rising demand for AI-generated videos is fueled by advances in large-scale Text-to-Video (T2V) models, trained on extensive datasets of video clips spanning diverse resolutions and durations. To address this data heterogeneity, current training methods often use a bucketing strategy that groups samples into discrete buckets for efficiency. However, this approach struggles to scale with compute and data volumes under static parallelism schemes, such as data and sequence parallelism, leading to significant workload imbalances and hardware under-utilization.
  In this paper, we present Arachne, a novel training framework for efficient T2V model training at scale. Arachne decomposes the training process into fine-grained computational units, called \textit{cascades}, orchestrating their distributed execution and synchronization across the cluster through coordinated spatial and temporal optimization. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Arachne reduces iteration time by up to 65\% over leading frameworks, exhibiting a positive scaling trend where its performance advantages amplify as training scale grows.

</details>


### [5] [Exploiting Task-Based Parallelism for the Red-Black Gauss-Seidel Method on 2D Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01735)
*Shiting Long,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo,Andreas Frommer,Dirk Pleiter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文实现了基于任务编程模型的红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，与传统分治并行实现相比，任务化方法能在保持相当性能的同时，更好地应对硬件级异步性，为多着色方案提供了更高效的并行化途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分治并行策略在实现多着色高斯-赛德尔方法时存在全局同步要求和负载不均衡的问题，导致执行效率低下。任务编程模型可以通过启用细粒度并行性、消除全局障碍以及允许不同颜色的更新在时间上部分重叠来缓解这些问题。

Method: 使用两种基于任务的编程模型实现红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，并将其与经典的分治并行实现进行比较，以评估细粒度并行性对执行效率的影响。选择红黑方案作为代表性示例，因为基于任务的方法可以自然扩展到非结构化网格和更宽模板产生的更一般的多着色方案。以二维泊松方程的求解作为基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于任务的实现可以达到与传统分治并行化相当的性能，同时提供对硬件级异步性更强的适应能力。

Conclusion: 基于任务的编程模型是实现多着色高斯-赛德尔方法的有效替代方案，能够在保持性能的同时提供更好的异步容错能力，特别适用于需要细粒度并行性的复杂网格和模板计算。

Abstract: Gauss-Seidel is a well-established iterative method for the solution of linear systems, and multicoloring has been widely used to increase parallelism in iterative solution techniques. Implementing multi-color Gauss-Seidel with conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization strategies, however, may be inefficient due to global synchronization requirements and load imbalances. Task-based programming models can mitigate these issues by enabling fine-grained parallelism, removing global barriers and allowing updates of different colors to partially overlap in time. In this work, we implement the red-black Gauss-Seidel method using two task-based programming models and compare them with a classical divide-and-conquer parallel implementation to evaluate the impact of fine-grained parallelism on execution efficiency. The red-black scheme serves as a representative example, as task-based approaches naturally extend to more general multi-color schemes arising from unstructured grids and wider stencils. Using the solve of the 2D Poisson equation as benchmark, our results show that task-based implementations can achieve performance comparable to conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization while providing greater resilience to hardware-level asynchronicity.

</details>


### [6] [HCMS: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline for Communication-Computation Overlap in Long-Sequence Parallel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01817)
*Chao Yuan,Pan Li,Yingnan Sun,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HCMS方法，通过多头注意力分块与双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠，在视频生成典型序列长度下相比Ulysses和Ring Attention分别提升10%-17.5%和5%-14.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全连接序列并行方法在处理中长序列时通信与计算严格串行执行，导致硬件资源利用率不足。

Method: 提出Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS)：利用多头注意力的计算独立性，将注意力头划分为多个块，通过双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠；与FlashAttention、SDPA等现有优化正交兼容，无需修改底层kernel，支持非均匀划分且保持数值等价性。

Result: 在四类GPU平台的2-8卡规模实验中验证：对于视频生成典型序列长度（31K-56K tokens），HCMS相比Ulysses基线提升10%-17.5%，相比Ring Attention提升5%-14.5%；Wan2.2模型端到端加速6.8%。理论分析表明收益与通信比率ρ正相关，建议在ρ>20%时使用。

Conclusion: HCMS通过细粒度流水线有效解决了中长序列处理中的资源利用瓶颈，为视频生成等长序列场景提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: All-to-all based sequence parallelism methods execute communication and computation strictly in serial when processing medium-long sequences, resulting in hardware resource underutilization. This paper proposes Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS), which exploits the computational independence of multi-head attention by partitioning attention heads into multiple chunks and achieving fine-grained communication-computation overlap through dual CUDA streams. HCMS is orthogonally compatible with existing optimizations such as FlashAttention and SDPA, requires no modification to underlying kernels, supports uneven partitioning while maintaining numerical equivalence. Experiments validate the effectiveness across four GPU platforms at 2-8 GPU scales: for typical video generation sequence lengths of 31K-56K tokens, HCMS achieves 10\%-17.5\% speedup over the Ulysses baseline and 5\%-14.5\% speedup over Ring Attention; end-to-end acceleration of 6.8\% is achieved on the Wan2.2 model. Theoretical analysis shows that HCMS benefits are positively correlated with communication ratio $ρ$, and its use is recommended when $ρ>20\%$.

</details>


### [7] [Lynx: Progressive Speculative Quantization for accelerating KV Transfer in Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01831)
*Wenchen Han,Gingfung Matthew Yeung,Marco Barletta,William Toner,Amory Hoste,Adam Barker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lynx通过将KV缓存分割为高优先级Anchor流和低优先级Residual流，实现了渐进式传输，在保持高精度推理的同时显著降低了首次令牌时间（TTFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理在LLM服务中日益普遍，需要在分离式推理架构中传输大量KV缓存，现有KV量化技术无法同时实现低网络延迟和高推理精度。传统方法假设KV缓存必须完整接收后才能使用，这一限制成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 挑战KV缓存作为不可分割单元的假设，利用不同比特对注意力计算贡献不等的特性，将KV缓存分割为携带最重要比特的高优先级Anchor流和携带剩余精度的低优先级Residual流。解码在收到Anchor流后立即开始，同时传输Residual流，最后通过验证确保与高精度解码等效。

Result: Lynx在多个模型和服务工作负载上实现了与激进4比特KV量化相当的TTFT，同时匹配高精度（BF16）推理的准确率。相比标准8比特KV量化，TTFT提升高达1.43倍；相比最先进方法，准确率提升高达5.1%。

Conclusion: 通过打破KV缓存必须完整接收的限制，采用渐进式分割流传输策略，Lynx成功实现了低延迟与高精度的平衡，为长上下文LLM服务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly common in large language model (LLM) serving, driven by retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems. In disaggregated inference, these workloads require transferring large Key-Value (KV) caches across the network, where decoding cannot begin until the transfer completes. Recent KV quantization techniques reduce data volume and alleviate this bottleneck, but existing schemes fail to achieve both low network-exposed latency and high inference accuracy.
  We challenge the assumption that the KV cache is an indivisible unit that must be fully received before use. We leverage the observation that different bits in the KV cache contribute unequally to attention computation and inference precision: the most significant bits capture the coarse structure of attention and the least significant bits refine precision. This property enables partial use of the KV cache during decoding. We present Lynx, a system that enables progressive, split-stream KV transfer by partitioning the KV cache into a high-priority Anchor stream carrying the most significant bits and a low-priority Residual stream carrying remaining precision. Decoding begins upon receipt of the Anchor stream and proceeds speculatively while the Residual stream is transferred concurrently, followed by verification that ensures equivalence to higher-precision decoding.
  Across multiple models and serving workloads, Lynx achieves Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) comparable to aggressive 4-bit KV quantization, while matching the accuracy of high-precision (BF16) inference, improving TTFT over standard 8-bit KV quantization by up to $1.43\times$ and improving accuracy over state-of-the-art by up to $5.1\%$.

</details>


### [8] [Mixture-of-Parallelisms: Towards Memory-Efficient Training Stack for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01844)
*Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Shrey Pandit,Yiran Zhao,Semih Yavuz,Silvio Savarese,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MoP，一种面向万亿参数MoE模型的高效内存训练范式，仅需不到12个8x H200 GPU节点即可实现百万token上下文长度的无损训练，相比FSDP2基线实现4.7x-8.2x的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MoE模型规模扩展至万亿参数，现有训练系统面临严重的内存瓶颈问题，特别是在处理超长上下文（百万token级别）时，传统的并行化策略无法充分利用硬件资源，导致显存不足和训练效率低下。

Method: 提出一种内存高效的训练范式，通过在MoE模型训练流水线的不同层级和阶段组合并特化现有与新型并行化技术，根据CPU、CPU内存、GPU HBM内存以及CPU-GPU、GPU-GPU、节点间通信带宽等物理约束优化资源分配；同时引入一种新颖的优化器步策略以实现高吞吐量和内存效率。

Result: 实验表明，MoP在单GPU吞吐量上比经过强优化的FSDP2基线提升4.7x-8.2x（规模越大提升越显著），并能支持高达1M token的上下文长度训练，而基线方法在64K-128K token时即出现显存溢出。

Conclusion: MoP成功实现了在有限硬件资源下对超大规模MoE模型的高效训练，突破了传统方法在长上下文场景下的内存限制，为大模型训练提供了一种实用的高效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper showcases a memory-efficient training stack for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. It is a training paradigm that combines and specializes various existing and novel parallelism techniques at different layers and stages of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model training pipeline. It leverages these techniques to achieve maximal efficiency given the physical constraints of CPU, CPU memory, GPU HBM memory, and the CPU-GPU, GPU-GPU, and node-node communication bandwidth of the GPU cluster. It also contains a novel strategy for the optimizer step to achieve high throughput and memory efficiency, enabling practitioners to conduct lossless pre-training/fine-tuning of trillion-parameter scale models, at a million context length, with just under 12 8x H200 GPU nodes, with state-of-the-art throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, MoP delivers 4.7x--8.2x higher per-GPU throughput than a strongly-tuned FSDP2 baseline (with the gap widening at larger scale) and sustains training at context lengths up to 1M tokens, where the baseline runs out of memory beyond 64--128K.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Load-Aware Prefill Deflection for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02043)
*Shrikara Arun,Anjaly Parayil,Srikant Bharadwaj,Renee St. Amant,Victor Rühle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动前缀偏转调度器，通过让解码节点以分块前缀步骤的方式处理请求的前缀阶段，解决了分离式LLM服务中预填充节点饱和而解码节点计算利用率不足的问题，显著降低了尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分离式LLM服务将预填充和解码阶段运行在独立的GPU池上，但在突发、重尾工作负载下会出现新的不对称性：预填充节点达到饱和，而解码节点计算资源未被充分利用。在2P2D配置的生产级A100集群上，预填充执行仅占P95首次令牌时间(TTFT)的2-23%，排队和节点间KV缓存传输占其余时间。

Method: 提出主动前缀偏转调度器：让解码节点以分块前缀步骤形式处理请求的前缀阶段，与正在进行的解码批次交错执行。对每个排队请求，估计其在预填充节点上的TTFT，并在每个解码节点上搜索最大的分块调度方案，使正在进行的解码保持在令牌间隔时间(TBT)SLO范围内，当解码路径有助于改善尾部延迟时进行偏转。偏转请求的前缀阶段在解码节点本地执行，消除了节点间KV传输。

Result: 在vLLM上实现，使用DeepSeek-V2-Lite在生产级轨迹上评估：P95 TTFT降低高达81%，SLO达成率提高高达79%，每个请求的路由开销为亚毫秒级。

Conclusion: 通过前缀偏转调度，可以在保持解码服务质量的同时有效利用解码节点的空闲计算资源，大幅改善分离式LLM服务的尾部延迟性能，且开销极低。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving runs prefill and decode on separate GPU pools to keep the two phases from interfering. In practice, this creates a new asymmetry: under bursty, heavy-tailed workloads prefill nodes saturate while decode nodes have compute underutilized, and on a production-style A100 cluster with 2 prefill and 2 decode nodes (2P2D), we find that prefill execution accounts for only 2-23% of P95 Time-to-First-Token (TTFT). Queuing and inter-node GPU-GPU KV-cache transfer account for the rest. We present a proactive prefill-deflecting scheduler that lets decode nodes serve prefill phase of requests as chunked-prefill steps interleaved with their in-flight decode batches. For each queued request, we estimate the TTFT it would see on the prefill node, and on every decode node, search for the largest chunk schedule that keeps in-flight decodes within their Time-Between-Tokens (TBT) SLO and deflect when the decode path helps tail latency. Because the prefill phase of deflected requests runs in place on the decode node, the inter-node KV transfer is eliminated. Implemented on vLLM and evaluated on production-style traces with DeepSeek-V2-Lite, our approach reduces P95 TTFT by upto 81% and raises SLO attainment by upto 79% over state-of-the-art disaggregated schedulers, at sub-millisecond per-request routing cost.

</details>


### [10] [Cadence: Extreme Pipelining with Multiple Concurrent Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02275)
*Fatima Elsheimy,Mohammad Mussadiq Jalalzai,Tobias Klenze,Jovan Komatovic,Mike Setrin,Victor Shoup,Kushal Babel,Lioba Heimbach,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cadence是首个实现多提议者共识(MCP)的拜占庭容错协议，提供短期抗审查和隐藏特性，具有任意低区块间隔、最优容错和最优快速路径延迟，最终确认平均仅需219毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识协议存在单领导者垄断交易纳入和排序的问题，且区块间隔受网络延迟限制。本研究旨在实现极端流水线化以解耦区块间隔与网络延迟，同时消除单领导者垄断，提供短期抗审查和隐藏特性。

Method: 提出将时间划分为等间隔槽位的框架，每个槽位对应一个区块，各共识实例独立运行。引入三个核心组件：(1)通用框架将一次性共识转换为多轮协议；(2)Chorus协议实现三轮快速路径最终化；(3)Conductor协调器以均匀节奏开放槽位。采用多并发提议者机制，每个区块由多个验证者提议。

Result: 在Monad的200个验证者环境中测试，每槽位5个提议者，最终确认平均219毫秒（推测最终性167毫秒），100毫秒区块间隔下交易平均等待50毫秒即可进入提案。实现了最优容错性(n=3f+1)下的安全性、活性、抗审查和隐藏特性的形式化证明。

Conclusion: Cadence成功实现了多提议者共识的重大突破，是首个在单领导者共识的快速路径延迟水平上提供短期抗审查和隐藏特性的MCP协议，显著提升了区块链系统的性能与公平性。

Abstract: We present Cadence, a Byzantine fault-tolerant multi-proposer consensus protocol with arbitrarily low block intervals, optimal resilience, and optimal fast-path latency. Cadence divides time into equally spaced slots, one block per slot, each finalized in its own consensus instance. Blocks do not build directly on their predecessor, so instances run independently and none waits for an earlier block to finish or propagate; we call this extreme pipelining, decoupling the block interval from network latency. Cadence also removes the single-leader monopoly over transaction inclusion and ordering: under multiple concurrent proposers (MCP), several validators propose for each block, and it guarantees that, under synchrony, a transaction a correct proposer includes cannot be censored or deferred (short-term censorship resistance), and that no proposer can craft its proposal in reaction to the others' (hiding). To realize extreme pipelining, we introduce a general framework that turns any one-shot consensus meeting our slot-consensus specification into a multi-shot protocol. We instantiate it for MCP with two protocols of our own: Chorus, a slot consensus whose fast path finalizes a block in an optimal three rounds, with speculative finality one round earlier, and Conductor, an orchestrator that opens slots at an even cadence, more slowly under asynchrony to keep open slots bounded. To our knowledge, Cadence is the first MCP protocol to provide short-term censorship resistance and hiding at the fast-path latency of single-leader consensus. We prove safety, liveness, censorship resistance, and hiding under partial synchrony with optimal resilience (n = 3f+1). In simulation over Monad's 200 validators with five proposers per slot, finalization averages 219 ms (167 ms to speculative finality); at a 100 ms block interval a transaction waits on average 50 ms to enter a proposal.

</details>


### [11] [Securing People and their Machines Against Major Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02304)
*Ohad Eitan,Idit Keidar,Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向基层平台的容错恢复机制，通过社会图、身份监护者和状态监护者实现私钥和设备丢失后的安全恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基层平台（由自选公钥标识的人及其智能设备组成的分布式系统）缺乏全局资源用于故障恢复，面临私钥丢失和设备损坏等重大故障时无法恢复的挑战。

Method: 提出基于基层社会图的点对点恢复方案：(1) 建立朋友关系的社会图；(2) 身份监护者（由用户指定）；(3) 状态监护者（平台特定）。身份恢复需身份监护者超级多数同意，朋友协助替换公钥并恢复关系；设备恢复仅需状态监护者协助。使用守护多智能体原子事务形式化规范，并通过通信意志智能体实现。

Result: 实现了安全社会图，证明了该实现能将可恢复故障的执行映射到规范的正确执行。类似方法应用于基层货币和债券系统，货币的单写入者日志可精确恢复，恢复后的主权可继续运行而无双花问题。

Conclusion: 该方案为无全局资源的分布式系统提供了有效的容错恢复机制，实现了身份和状态的安全恢复，具有形式化正确性保证，并可扩展至不同类型的基层应用。

Abstract: We consider grassroots platforms -- distributed systems of agents consisting of people identified by self-chosen public keys and their machines (smartphones) -- and wish to make them secure against \emph{major faults}: the loss of their private keys and/or their smartphones. As grassroots platforms have no global resource to rely on for recovery, our peer-based solution is based on: (\ia) \emph{a grassroots social graph} in which agents establish and maintain friendships; (\ib) \emph{identity custodians}, designated by each person, and (\ic) \emph{state custodians}, which are grassroots platform-specific. Upon a person experiencing identity loss, and given a willing supermajority of the identity custodians of the person, the friends of the person replace the old public key with the new one across the graph and restore friendships, where all friends serve as state custodians for the social graph. Choosing a new keypair, obtaining a new smartphone, and convincing identity custodians to will a change of key all happen ``off-chain''. Recovery from machine loss without loss of key (e.g. smartphone run over by truck, or its memory wiped) is simpler, requiring only the help of state custodians.
  We specify the social graph and its secure version as guarded multiagent atomic transactions, and implement the secure social graph via communicating volitional agents, an eventually synchronous message-passing model one step closer to implementation. We prove the implementation maps runs with recoverable faults to correct runs of the specification.
  We follow a similar path for grassroots coins and bonds, showing a common core as well as the platform-specific aspects of state recovery: a currency's single-writer log is recovered exactly, the recovered sovereign resuming without double-spending.

</details>


### [12] [Elasticity in Parallel Sparse Triangular Solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02324)
*Raphael S. Steiner,Christos K. Matzoros,Pál András Papp,Toni Böhnlein,A. N. Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了在并行稀疏三角线性系统求解中使用陈旧同步并行模式，并设计了ElasticDivide调度器，在48核ARM和x86机器上分别实现了7-30%和19-60%的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行稀疏三角线性系统求解中，传统的同步调度器存在同步开销大的问题，限制了并行性能。为了减少同步等待时间，提高并行效率，需要一种新的执行模式来重叠同步和计算操作。

Method: 提出了陈旧同步并行(Stale Synchronous Parallel)执行模式，允许同步和计算操作重叠执行；设计了一个通用的有向无环图(DAG)调度器ElasticDivide，能够生成这种陈旧同步并行调度。该方法通过允许一定程度的陈旧数据来换取更高的并行度。

Result: 在48核ARM机器上，ElasticDivide相比最先进的同步调度器GrowLocal实现了7-30%的几何平均加速；在48核x86机器上，相比SpMP实现了19-60%的几何平均加速。

Conclusion: 陈旧同步并行模式能够有效减少并行稀疏三角线性系统求解中的同步开销，通过重叠同步和计算操作显著提升了并行性能，证明了该方法在多核架构上的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce stale synchronous parallel as a mode of execution in parallel sparse triangular linear system solve and present a general directed-acyclic-graph scheduler capable of producing such schedules. Stale-synchronous-parallel schedules allow the overlap of synchronisation and compute which results in a geometric-mean speed-up of $7$-$30\%$ of our scheduler, ElasticDivide, over state-of-the-art synchronous scheduler GrowLocal on an ARM machine using 48 cores. On an x86 machine using 48 cores, we report geometric-mean speed-ups of $19$-$60\%$ over SpMP.

</details>


### [13] [WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02391)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了WattGPU，一个无需硬件访问或性能分析即可预测LLM在GPU上功耗和延迟的框架。该方法仅利用公开的LLM元数据和GPU规格，在未见过的GPU和LLM上实现了功耗预测误差≤13.5%、延迟预测误差≤8.5%，相比传统基准方法误差降低约2-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理工作负载正成为数据中心能耗的重要来源。优化部署需要将特定LLM匹配到最高效的GPU，但运营商目前缺乏在不进行详尽性能分析的情况下实现这一目标的工具。现有的预测模型仍需要性能分析数据，且难以泛化到训练期间未见过的硬件。

Method: 提出WattGPU框架，包含两个预测模型：平均GPU功耗模型和Token间延迟（ITL）模型。该方法仅利用公开可用的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问或性能分析，能够泛化到未见过的NVIDIA服务器级GPU和LLM。使用留一GPU和留一LLM交叉验证进行评估，数据集包含42个开源LLM（0.1B-27B参数）和8个GPU，涵盖离线和服务器两种场景。

Result: 功耗模型在未见过的GPU上离线场景中达到中位绝对百分比误差≤3.4%，服务器场景≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式下达到≤8.5%。两种模型在服务器场景下均保持强GPU排序相关性（Kendall τ≥0.76）。与传统的基于物理的基准（功耗用负载缩放TDP，延迟用roofline模型）相比，在服务器场景下未见过的LLM-GPU组合误差降低约4倍，对完全未见过的GPU误差降低约2倍。

Conclusion: WattGPU成功实现了在无需硬件访问和性能分析的情况下准确预测LLM在GPU上的功耗和延迟性能，为数据中心运营商提供了有效的工具来优化LLM部署和能耗管理。数据和代码已公开可用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.

</details>


### [14] [FlintKV: A Fast Durable Storage Engine for Modern Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02401)
*Sergey Egorov,Gregory Chockler,Brijesh Dongol,Dan O'Keeffe,Sadegh Keshavarzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlintKV，一个基于跳表的NVM优化键值存储引擎，原生支持生产级KV存储的完整API（快照、一致性迭代器、原子批处理），通过创新的扁平合并并发控制算法，在保证持久化线性一致性的同时，端到端吞吐量相比现有工作提升高达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NVM键值存储虽然在写入和点查询方面表现优异，但缺乏或未充分支持现代数据库所需的丰富接口保障。生产级键值引擎（如RocksDB）提供的即时快照、一致性迭代器和原子批处理特性对于实现事务和并发控制至关重要，但现有NVM存储方案对此支持不足。

Method: FlintKV采用基于跳表的架构，核心是一个新颖的基于扁平合并（flat-combining）的并发控制算法。该算法结合多版本控制机制和精心协同设计的持久化机制，同时支持原子批处理写入和快照一致性迭代，并保证持久化线性一致性。系统可独立部署，其持久化跳表组件也可集成到现有NVM存储中。

Result: 实验评估表明，FlintKV在端到端吞吐量上相比现有工作可获得高达75%的性能提升，同时能够高效支持原子批处理写入和快照一致性迭代功能。

Conclusion: FlintKV成功填补了NVM键值存储在生产级功能支持方面的空白，证明了在NVM环境下可以同时实现高性能和完整的ACID特性支持，为现代数据库系统的存储引擎设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Byte-addressable non-volatile memory (NVM) offers an opportunity to rethink storage engine architectures. While recent NVM key-value stores achieve high throughput for ingestion and point lookups, they omit or under-specify the support for the richer interface guarantees required by modern databases. Production key-value engines (e.g., RocksDB) provide point-in-time snapshots, consistent iterators, and atomic batches-features essential for implementing transactions and concurrency control. We present FlintKV, an NVM-optimized skiplist-based storage engine that natively supports the full API of production key-value stores. FlintKV supports both atomic batch writes and snapshot-consistent iteration efficiently while guaranteeing durable linearizability. FlintKV can be deployed standalone or its durable skiplist can be integrated into existing NVM stores to enhance their capabilities. Central to FlintKV is a novel flat-combining based concurrency control algorithm that leverages multi-versioning and carefully co-designed persistence mechanisms to ensure high performance and scalability. Our empirical evaluation shows that FlintKV can achieve up to a 75% improvement in end-to-end throughput over prior work.

</details>

### [15] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M-QCDNet模型，通过Q矩阵结构先验将认知诊断模型的可解释性与神经网络相结合，在保持预测性能的同时确保潜在掌握状态的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知诊断模型虽具有结构可解释性但灵活性不足，深度学习神经网络虽灵活但缺乏可解释性，两者之间存在难以兼顾预测性能与结构一致性的问题。

Method: 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入神经网络以构建项目-技能关系；设计带L2惩罚项的损失函数，惩罚与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活，平衡预测性能与结构对齐；开发可解释的对齐度量指标以量化预测技能激活与项目级技能的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet确保潜在掌握状态保持可解释且符合认知理论；提出的评估矩阵能量化预测技能激活与项目级技能的对应程度；模型在预测性能与结构对齐之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet通过将诊断有效性嵌入模型设计，成功连接心理测量透明性与神经灵活性，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI发展，能为课堂实践提供学习困难早期检测和基于掌握的干预支持。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [16] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出I²RMAN方法，通过频率特异性黎曼流形建模和频率聚类聚合，解决跨被试EEG压力检测中的挑战，在三个数据集上达到82.78%的准确率，参数效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG压力检测面临挑战，因为与压力相关的判别性模式既依赖于被试又具有频率特异性。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽视了对高级认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡，而标准的时间标记化方法往往会破坏切片间的时间连贯性。

Method: 提出I²RMAN（Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network）：1）在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何和频率特异性判别线索；2）引入频率聚类聚合，选择信息丰富的频谱分量并通过形成紧凑的数据驱动频率簇来减少冗余；3）设计切片内-切片间注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态和全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，I²RMAN持续优于五个最先进的基线方法，实现了高达82.78%的平衡准确率，同时保持高效性，仅有1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: I²RMAN成功地将频率特异性的黎曼几何建模与注意力机制相结合，有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中频率特异性模式提取和时间连贯性保持的问题，在性能和效率方面都取得了显著成果。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [17] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程ByExample(PBE)系统在对抗性攻击下的脆弱性，提出了一种基于版本空间划分聚合(VPA)的防御方法，并通过实验验证了在低边缘PBE任务中对抗性鲁棒性是传统噪声评估无法捕捉的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的鲁棒性PBE研究通常将错误示例建模为随机噪声过程，然后最小化期望或经验损失。但本文关注一种不同的失败模式：存在一个对抗者，他能够观察合成器并选择性地破坏示例，从而最大程度地损坏返回的程序。作者发现低边缘PBE任务存在一个被随机打字错误和噪声PBE评估所忽视的对抗性鲁棒性维度。

Method: 1. 形式化了有限PBE版本空间的固定集合最坏情况破坏问题；2. 为字符串转换DSL实现了精确有界池内搜索和启发式破坏搜索；3. 提出了版本空间划分聚合(VPA)防御方法——在不相交的示例组上进行合成，并通过语义签名进行投票。实验设计包括：策划/生成的DSL任务、公开的SyGuS PBE_SLIA切片、SYNTRA Playgol v2以及噪声PBE目标基线。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 一个精心策划的编辑可以翻转所有8个尖峰任务，而200次试验的打字错误、DSL池和距离匹配的随机控制成功率分别为10.3%、11.0%和16.7%；2) 在生成的边缘-1行上，预算1即可翻转但VPA能恢复它们；3) 在公开SyGuS上，投票边缘接近1，自适应攻击者将VPA准确率降至零；4) 公开SyGuS切片跨越精确池内预算边界移动；5) Playgol在141个接受行上显示出相对于打字错误和同池随机控制的正成对Bootstrap差距；6) 在20个受控边缘-1任务上的小规模精确输出提示测试显示，本地和API模型都呈现相同的从清洁到被攻击的定性模式。

Conclusion: 研究得出一个明确的边界结论：低边缘PBE任务确实存在对抗性鲁棒性维度，而语义划分聚合仅在清洁语义保持划分投票边缘时才有帮助，这通常在现实任务中会失败。该发现强调了对抗性攻击是PBE系统安全评估中不可忽视的重要因素。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [18] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于ECG识别，通过融合PRQST关键点作为领域知识并建模时空关系，在九分类ECG数据集上达到88.1%的平均F1分数，特别是对稀有类别取得76.3%的F1分数，优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能发展迅速，但AI模型的可解释性仍是挑战，尤其在心电图（ECG）识别等专业医疗领域。传统的端到端卷积神经网络缺乏可解释性，需要一种融合领域知识的方法来提升模型性能和可解释性。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于ECG识别。将ECG解读中至关重要的PRQST关键点作为领域知识融入模型。采用双流有向图建模ECG周期内部和周期间的关系：空间有向图捕获关键点之间的位置关系，时间有向图描述扩展ECG序列中相邻周期之间的时间依赖性。

Result: 在First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition数据集上进行九分类ECG识别实验。总体平均F1分数为88.1%，稀有类别平均F1分数为76.3%，均优于现有最先进模型。领域知识的引入显著提升了检测性能，特别是对稀有类别的识别效果。

Conclusion: 领域知识的引入能够有效提升ECG识别性能，特别是对稀有类别的检测。所提出的双流有向图方法能够有效建模ECG的时空特征，证明了在医疗AI应用中融合领域知识的重要价值。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [19] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的扩展性能，发现该方法在GloVe嵌入数据集上具有维度鲁棒性优势，索引成本低，查询扩展性近似线性，适用于重建频繁或高维场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近似最近邻搜索扩展性分析中缺乏对基于网格方法的系统性研究，而网格方法可能在高维和重建频繁场景中具有潜在优势。此外，自注意力机制已被形式化为ANN操作，ANN算法的扩展性特性可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统性扩展性分析，研究其在数据集规模N和维度d变化时的性能表现。通过实验对比多探针网格方法与基于图、树和划分的其他ANN方法，在GloVe嵌入数据集上进行维度扩展性分析。

Result: 在GloVe嵌入数据集上发现了一个未报道过的维度扩展性交叉现象：多探针网格搜索保持了近似常数的维度扩展指数，而基于图、树和划分的方法吞吐量出现退化。该方法具有近似线性的N扩展性和比竞争ANN方法更低的索引成本。

Conclusion: 多探针网格方法在重建频繁或高维场景中（索引成本和维度鲁棒性决定性能）具有竞争力。鉴于自注意力可形式化为ANN操作，ANN算法的N和d扩展性特性可指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [20] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IonSense-QKG框架，通过量子相关元数据增强锂离子电池数据集，并引入量子准备度评分，为混合量子-经典机器学习工作流提供数据集选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共锂离子电池数据集在化学成分、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构、访问状态和预处理复杂度等方面存在显著差异，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，缺乏针对量子计算场景的数据集评估标准。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，提出量子准备度元数据框架，为公共电池数据集记录增加量子相关元数据（包括任务类型、感知模态、化学成分、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围和NISQ可行性），并引入透明的量子准备度评分来排序数据集。

Result: 框架展示了基于查询的发现能力，能够识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测和未来电池健康基准测试的数据集，发布了包括元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的完整工具集。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，为未来混合量子-经典电池基准测试提供了数据集选择启发式方法。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [21] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维和多项逻辑回归分类的新方法，用于DNA甲基化数据的中枢神经系统肿瘤分类，在独立临床队列上达到86%的分类准确率和93%的家族水平准确率，相比现有最佳方法分别提升约4和5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DNA甲基化分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的有力手段，但在跨队列可迁移性、方法学正确性以及稳健的多分类评估方面仍存在重要挑战，现有分类器性能有待提升。

Method: 提出一种新的机器学习方法，采用稀疏随机投影进行维度约简，结合多项逻辑回归进行分类，在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 在2801个样本的参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率达96%；在1104个样本的独立临床评估队列上，91个类别水平的准确率达86%，甲基化类别家族水平准确率达93%，相比现有最佳参考数据（82%类别水平和88%家族水平一致性）分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该基于更强机器学习方法实践构建的模型在各项评估设置中持续优于先前最佳水平，可实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对诊断中的肿瘤亚型分配和治疗选择具有重要临床意义。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [22] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书从经典理论（近似、优化、泛化）到当代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成模型、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐与涌现）构建了一个统一、面向证明的现代深度学习理论体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越任何单一数学解释，现有理论分散且缺乏统一框架。作者旨在整合分散的研究成果，提供一个连贯的研究叙事，厘清各理论的控制对象、假设条件和未解释现象。

Method: 组织广泛文献形成连贯研究叙事：针对每个理论，分析其控制的对象、使其成立的假设条件，以及该理论无法解释的现象。从经典基础（近似、优化、泛化）延伸至当代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成模型、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐与涌现）。

Result: 构建了现代深度学习理论的严格图谱，揭示理论现状：强大但不完整，且日益聚焦于"学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现"这一核心问题。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一、严谨的深度学习理论框架，展示了理论如何从经典基础发展到当代涌现机制，为理解学习机制的起源提供了系统性视角。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [23] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NightVision攻击方法，通过受限的黑盒API访问，估算LLM的隐藏层维度、深度和参数量，证明当前API限制不足以完全掩盖模型架构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明，通过有限的API访问（top-k logits和logit bias功能）可以恢复LLM的架构细节。因此大多数商业LLM提供商限制了API，只暴露单个解码token的logit。本文旨在研究在当前受限API下是否仍能恢复模型架构参数。

Method: 提出了NightVision攻击方法，采用两个核心技术：1）通用集合提示（common set prompting）技术，通过多个提示暴露同一组输出token的对数概率，并通过频谱分析推断隐藏层维度；2）利用端到端首token时间（TTFT）测量和估计的隐藏维度来估算模型深度和参数量。该方法仅使用受限的黑盒API访问。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上进行实证评估：隐藏维度平均相对误差为23%（MoE模型为9%）；对于超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量的估计误差在53%以内。消融实验显示了准确率如何随token预算和模型特性扩展。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM API限制不足以完全掩盖其底层模型的架构细节，商业LLM提供商需要进一步考虑API访问的安全性问题。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [24] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个AI管道系统，利用多模态数据（铁路道口图像和事故报告）进行安全性评估，能识别高低风险道口(F1=0.757)并估算FRA安全分数(RMSE=0.078)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用铁路道口的视觉线索和结构化事故历史数据，构建能与专家意见和美国联邦铁路管理局(FRA)安全评分对齐的AI系统，实现自动化的道口安全评估。

Method: 提出一个概念验证管道系统，采用路由微调的紧凑视觉语言模型(VLM)架构，整合多模态数据，涵盖数据准备到不同学习范式的完整流程。

Result: 在高低风险道口分类任务上达到宏观F1分数0.757；在估算FRA安全分数方面实现RMSE为0.078，相关性为0.492，且定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了基于多模态数据的铁路道口安全评估系统原型，证明了融合视觉信息和结构化历史数据的有效性，为智能交通安全监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [25] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer中数值编码策略，发现混合token方法（保留数值但分箱后投影）是最实用的默认选择，其最佳分箱数遵循数据集大小的幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)数据中的数值编码缺乏系统性研究，需要明确不同编码策略在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间的权衡，以指导实际应用中的方法选择。

Method: 系统比较离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，使用嵌入真实EHR数据的合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务进行评估，分析数值精度、优化稳定性与架构灵活性的权衡关系。

Result: 显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感的算术任务中表现最佳；混合token方法（保留数值但分箱后投影）更稳健通用，最优分箱数与数据集大小呈幂律关系；模型表现出"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术；实验室数值的临床收益因任务而异。

Conclusion: 实际应用中鲁棒性和可部署性往往优于最大数值精度，混合token方法应作为实际默认选择，为EHR数据中的数值编码提供了实用指导。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [26] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对四种代码智能体强化学习执行环境进行了对比研究，发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的差异，在百万轨迹训练中预计有1.8倍的工作时长差距，建议将执行环境作为训练系统的一部分进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码智能体强化学习研究将执行基础设施视为后台实现细节，忽略了对其开销的测量。然而在强化学习后训练中，大规模的交互式软件rollout使得每次rollout的微小节省会在规模上产生巨大累积效应，这是一个被错失的优化机会。

Method: 对四种执行环境进行比较研究：单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器和云虚拟机。测量并分析各环境在冷启动延迟等方面的性能差异，并预测在百万级150步轨迹训练场景下的工作时长差异。

Result: 发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的差异，在百万条150步轨迹的训练场景中，预计工作时长存在1.8倍的差异范围。

Conclusion: 未来的代码智能体强化学习系统应该将执行环境作为训练系统本身的一部分进行优化，而不仅仅是将其视为部署管道。执行基础设施的选择和优化对系统效率具有重要影响。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [27] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了NeuroBridge框架，这是一个临床指导的多任务MRI分析框架，通过结合大规模自监督预训练与多任务学习，显著提升了阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症的诊断准确性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MRI准确识别阿尔茨海默病（AD）、轻度认知障碍（MCI）及相关痴呆症仍具有挑战性，因为疾病相关的结构变化通常微妙且异质性强，传统方法难以有效捕捉这些细微特征。

Method: 开发了NeuroBridge框架，该框架整合了大规模自监督MRI预训练，并结合海马体分割、海马体萎缩分类和重建目标等多任务学习，随后采用门控融合机制进行微调。研究在ADNI和OASIS队列中评估了性能，包括跨队列迁移、概率分析和机会性筛查。

Result: NeuroBridge在所有评估的分类任务中均取得了最佳性能，在ADNI数据集中AD与认知正常对照组的分类准确率达88.17%，在OASIS数据集中达82.78%。在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中提升最为显著。该框架展示了强大的跨队列泛化能力、预测类别概率与准确率之间的系统性关联，以及基于概率的机会性筛查的可行性。

Conclusion: 临床指导的多任务表示学习超越了传统单任务方法，提升了神经退行性疾病的MRI诊断效果。NeuroBridge为痴呆症评估和基于MRI的机会性筛查提供了一个稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [28] [DeadPool: Resilient LLM Training with Hot-Swapping via Zero-Overhead Checkpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01646)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Lishan Yang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DeadPool，一个针对大规模LLM训练的容错系统，通过热插拔机制实现了零开销的正常执行和快速故障恢复（<40秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的大语言模型训练需要数万张GPU运行数月，软硬件栈各层均会发生故障。现有容错机制要么在无故障执行时引入显著开销，要么恢复延迟过长，特别是在少数计算节点发生永久性故障的场景下。需要在无故障开销和恢复延迟之间寻求更优平衡。

Method: DeadPool采用热插换机制，用备用节点替换故障节点而无需终止整个作业。核心包含两个技术：(1) 利用非关键路径的内存检查点机制实现空间冗余；(2) 引入通信器重构协议在运行时用备用节点替换故障节点。该方法将内存检查点与计算重叠，实现零开销。

Result: 在最多512张NVIDIA A100 GPU和最大650亿参数的LLM上评估，实现了零检查点开销，热插换恢复在40秒内完成。实验证明DeadPool同时实现了零开销的无故障执行和极低的恢复成本。

Conclusion: DeadPool成功解决了LLM训练容错中的关键权衡问题，首次同时实现了零开销的错误无关执行和极低的恢复成本，为大规模分布式训练提供了高效可靠的容错解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language model (LLM) training takes tens of thousands of graphics processing units (GPUs) for months and encounters failures across the software and hardware stack. Existing fault-tolerance mechanisms either impose non-trivial overhead during failure-free execution or suffer from prolonged recovery latency, particularly under scenarios where a small subset of compute nodes experience permanent failures. %The tradeoff between failure-free overhead and recovery latency forms a space forms a Pareto frontier We present DeadPool to simultaneously address both optimization objectives. DeadPool incorporates a fault-tolerance mechanism that restores LLM training via hot-swapping, namely by replacing failed nodes with spare nodes without terminating the complete job. The hot-swapping of DeadPool is enabled by two ideas: First, it exploits an off-critical-path in-memory checkpointing mechanism for spatial redundancy. Second, it introduces a communicator reconstruction protocol that replaces failed nodes with spare nodes at runtime. DeadPool efficiently overlaps the in-memory checkpointing with computation, thus introducing zero overhead during error-free execution. Upon permanent node failures, DeadPool can rebuild memory states with minimal recomputation by leveraging in-memory checkpoints. We evaluate DeadPool across scales (up to 512 NVIDIA A100 GPUs) and LLMs (up to 65B parameters), and observe zero checkpoint overhead with hot-swapping recovery completing in under 40 seconds. These results show that DeadPool simultaneously achieves both zero-overhead error-free execution and extremely low recovery cost.

</details>


### [29] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpinGTP方法，通过引入自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)解决了Gaunt张量积(GTP)无法捕获反对称路径的问题，在保持GTP渐近效率的同时恢复了缺失的反对称相互作用，实现了与完整Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)相当的精度，特别是在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)等变网络在3D原子系统建模中很有前景，但Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)的O(L^6)复杂度限制了其可扩展性。近期提出的Gaunt张量积(GTP)虽然降低了复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 提出SpinGTP方法，从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)。利用SWSH的代数性质，恢复缺失的反对称相互作用，同时保持GTP的渐近效率。该方法还允许更具表达力的等变基，自然地考虑张量积的奇宇称分量。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试中评估SpinGTP。结果显示SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度。值得注意的是，通过显式捕获反对称路径，SpinGTP在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 本工作为实现大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，成功解决了GTP的不完整性问题，同时保持了计算效率。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [30] [SCAPE: Accurate and Efficient LLM Training with Extreme Sparse Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01678)
*Mingkai Zheng,Junlin Chen,Haotian Xie,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAPE，一种面向LLM预训练的通信高效分布式优化器，通过基于Adam一阶矩的稀疏化策略，在保持模型质量的同时实现高达99%的稀疏度，端到端预训练时间减少43.3%，单步加速达3.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM预训练中，通信开销逐渐成为主导成本，尤其在数据并行和分片训练方案下。现有通信优化方法存在局限性：基于原始梯度的稀疏化在现代Adam优化器下高稀疏度时不稳定；量化通信的收益受限于位宽且常引入额外运行开销。

Method: SCAPE利用Adam一阶矩的稳定性，从基于一阶矩的统计量而非原始梯度推导稀疏化掩码；将掩码生成在workers间分区以与优化器分片对齐；延迟一步使用掩码使掩码同步与计算重叠；从单个同步稀疏缓冲区重构二阶矩更新所需量，避免额外集合通信。

Result: 在Megatron-LM中实现SCAPE，使用32块NVIDIA GH200 GPU预训练GPT-345M和Llama-500M。在90%和99%稀疏度下，SCAPE保持训练稳定性、验证损失和下游任务准确率。Llama-500M端到端预训练时间减少43.3%；Llama-1.8B单步加速达3.26倍。

Conclusion: SCAPE成功解决了LLM分布式训练中的通信瓶颈问题，通过创新性地利用优化器状态进行稀疏化，在保持模型质量的同时实现显著的训练加速，为大规模LLM训练提供了有效的通信优化方案。

Abstract: Communication increasingly dominates the cost of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training, especially under data-parallel and sharded training schemes, where gradient synchronization and parameter reconstruction overhead increase with model size and system scale. Existing communication-reduction methods either sparsify raw gradients, which can be unstable for modern Adam-style optimizers at high sparsity, or quantize communication, whose savings are fundamentally bounded by bit width and often incur additional runtime overhead. We present SCAPE, a communication-efficient distributed optimizer for LLM training that exploits the stability of AdamS's first-moment to enable aggressive sparsification without loss of LLM quality. Instead of constructing masks from raw gradients, SCAPE derives them from first-moment-based statistics, partitions mask generation across workers to align with optimizer sharding, and delays mask usage by one step so that mask synchronization can overlap with computation. SCAPE also reconstructs the quantities required for second-moment updates from a single synchronized sparse buffer, avoiding an additional collective. We implement SCAPE in Megatron-LM and evaluate its convergence by pre-training GPT-345M on OpenWebText and Llama-500M on SlimPajama-6B using 32 NVIDIA GH200 GPUs on TACC Vista. In both models, SCAPE preserves training stability, validation loss, and downstream task accuracy under 90\% and 99\% sparsity. For Llama-500M, SCAPE reduces end-to-end pre-training wall-clock time by up to 43.3\% while maintaining model quality comparable to dense AdamW and AdamS. For Llama-1.8B, SCAPE achieves up to 3.26$\times$ speedup per step compared to dense AdamS.

</details>


### [31] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了条件推断树（CIT）和条件推断森林（CIF）作为下游预测任务中top-k特征排序方法的性能。研究发现，在固定节点上使用Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值可以控制节点级拒绝率。CIF在分类方法中排名第4（22个数据集，17种方法），在回归方法中排名第3（8个数据集，18种方法）。运行时消融实验表明，自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大，关闭自适应停止和使用精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，而下游分数变化最多为0.011。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推断树（CIT）和条件推断森林（CIF）通过在选择分割阈值之前测试特征来减少分割选择偏差，但重复的置换测试和阈值搜索使得这些方法计算成本高昂。因此需要系统研究CIT和CIF作为top-k特征排序方法的有效性和计算效率。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在固定节点上，如果特征和置换预算不依赖于节点响应，研究Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值在完全置换零假设下对节点级拒绝的控制能力
2. 实验验证：使用真实数据基准测试、运行时消融研究和合成特征恢复实验
3. 下游预测评估：在22个数据集上比较17种分类方法，在8个数据集上比较18种回归方法
4. 运行时消融：分析自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间的影响

Result: 1. 理论结果：Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值能够在完全置换零假设下控制节点级拒绝
2. 排名结果：CIF在分类方法中排名第4（17种方法，22个数据集），在回归方法中排名第3（18种方法，8个数据集）
3. 运行时消融：关闭自适应停止使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍，使用精确阈值搜索使拟合时间增加1.9-10.8倍，而下游分数变化最多为0.011
4. 特征采样问题：稀疏高p模拟表明，森林特征采样可能导致信息性特征被排除在许多分割决策之外

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将CIF作为下游预测基准测试中的top-k特征排序方法。CIF在保持良好性能的同时，通过自适应停止和近似阈值搜索可以显著减少计算成本。然而，在稀疏高维场景下，特征采样可能会导致信息特征被遗漏的问题需要关注。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>
