*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型（MoE）在领域特定专家剪枝下，任务效用与事实可靠性之间的权衡关系。研究发现适度剪枝可在生物医学领域内保持效用且不立即损害可靠性，但在极端剪枝或跨域场景下，幻觉风险与性能下降显著增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE专家剪枝研究主要关注基准测试的效用（utility），却忽视了剪枝对事实可靠性（reliability）的影响，特别是在生物医学等高风险领域。由于MoE模型需要加载整个网络而带来巨大内存需求，迫切需要研究如何在资源受限环境下安全地压缩部署，同时保证模型的可靠性。

Method: 作者对四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法及多种剪枝比例进行了系统性评估。实验涵盖生成任务和分类任务，分别在领域内（生物医学）和跨领域场景下进行测试，分析不同剪枝程度对模型效用和可靠性的双重影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：适度剪枝能够在领域内保持效用且不立即导致可靠性下降，但在极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险显著增加；当转向通用领域时，模型的效用和可靠性均快速退化。这说明安全的模型压缩高度依赖于任务和具体应用领域。

Conclusion: 仅基于效用指标评估剪枝后的MoE模型不足以满足高风险部署需求，必须纳入可靠性评估。模型压缩的安全性取决于具体任务和领域背景，强调了在生物医学等关键应用中进行全面评估的重要性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [33] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRS-KAN混合神经网络架构，将R函数引入KAN框架，通过解析编码几何约束显式表示不连续性和边界，测试RMSE降低高达67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络难以显式表示不连续性、可行域边界和几何约束，标准KAN框架缺乏对已知几何或逻辑约束的直接编码能力，导致在处理具有明确几何结构的问题时预测精度和边界定位能力受限。

Method: 将V.L.Rvachev的R函数集成到Kolmogorov-Arnold Network框架中，提出混合架构：KAN分支学习平滑非线性结构，可微R函数解析编码已知几何/逻辑约束。通过R-合取和R-析取实现可微逻辑操作，将复杂几何支撑解析表示并直接纳入回归模型。提出加性、乘性和不可知分支加权三种GRS-KAN变体。

Result: 在包含圆形和矩形支撑的不连续性回归问题测试中，几何感知的GRS-KAN模型相比标准KAN显著提升预测精度和边界定位能力，测试RMSE降低高达67%，同时通过学习到的几何结构的解析表示提高了可解释性。不可知变体能够自动判断几何先验对特定学习任务是否有益。

Conclusion: GRS-KAN成功实现了几何约束与神经网络的有机融合，证明了显式几何编码在提升模型精度、边界定位和可解释性方面的有效性，为处理具有几何结构的复杂回归问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [34] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ember优化器，专门针对语言模型中的嵌入层和LM-head进行优化，显著降低显存占用（从O(2VD)降至O(V+D)），同时保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中的嵌入表和LM-head作为离散符号与连续表示之间的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与密集隐藏权重不同。现有优化器（如Adam）对这些参数使用统一的优化策略，未能利用其特殊结构，导致不必要的显存开销。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，专门针对嵌入和LM-head矩阵设计。该方法利用这些层的特殊梯度几何特性，仅需O(V+D)的VRAM（相比Adam的O(2VD)），且无需分片token表的优化器状态。作者还发现token的优化轨迹可以用简单的1D射线描述。

Result: 实证表明Ember在批大小和参数数量上均能有效扩展。在监督微调、强化学习和预训练任务中，Ember改进了帕累托前沿，同时仅使用千字节级别的优化器状态。研究还揭示了神经网参数优化轨迹的简单性，反驳了其必须在高度非凸景观中导航的普遍观点。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对Transformer训练优化器设计的原理性理解，证明了针对特定参数类型设计专门优化器的价值。Ember作为轻量级解决方案，可无缝集成到现有的ZeRO/FSDP框架中，为大规模语言模型训练提供了更高效的优化选择。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [35] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCGNM优化器解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，通过分组动量归一化提升少数类性能，并设计FedHOO算法优化重采样率，在多个基准数据集上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临严重的类别不平衡挑战，代表性不足的类别预测性能较差，而由于隐私和异构性约束，无法使用标准的集中式技术来解决这个问题。

Method: 提出FedCGNM客户端优化器：基于最小组内方差将类别划分为少量组，每组维护一个动量，将每组动量归一化为单位长度，使用归一化组动量之和作为更新方向；同时引入基于X-armed-bandit的FedHOO算法，利用联邦并行性以线性成本高效优化小客户端场景下的重采样率。

Result: 在四个公共长尾基准和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上的实证评估表明，FedCGNM持续优于基线方法，在小规模联邦中FedHOO带来进一步的性能提升；该方法既均衡了多数类和少数类之间的梯度幅度，又减轻了稀有类梯度中固有的噪声。

Conclusion: FedCGNM有效解决了联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，通过创新的分组动量归一化机制和FedHOO优化算法，在保护隐私的前提下显著提升了少数类的预测性能，为实际应用（如芯片缺陷检测）提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [36] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个考虑模型大小和训练数据的三项扩展定律，能够用更少的训练运行恢复最优批次大小的缩放规律，并适用于次优批次大小的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩展定律需要大量训练运行来拟合，且未明确分离训练步骤和批次大小对模型性能的影响。研究者希望提出一个更高效的扩展定律，能够利用次优批次大小的训练运行，减少拟合所需的实验成本。

Method: 提出三项扩展定律（three-term law），显式地将训练数据分解为训练步骤和批次大小。通过在大量训练运行上拟合该定律，验证其对最优批次大小缩放规律的恢复能力，并展示其可用较少的训练运行进行稳健拟合。进一步推导次优批次大小的扩展定律，并与临界批次大小的经验发现进行对比。

Result: 三项扩展定律能够正确恢复最优批次大小的缩放规律；与现有方法相比，可用显著更少的训练运行稳健地拟合该定律；该定律还可推导次优批次大小的扩展规律，并与临界批次大小的经验发现相吻合。

Conclusion: 三项扩展定律为模型训练效率提升提供了理论和实践基础，通过显式建模批次大小与训练步骤的关系，能够更高效地预测最优训练配置，减少实验成本，并扩展了扩展定律的适用范围。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [37] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE方法，通过统一框架分解优势函数，实现自适应调整梯度权重，显著提升LLM推理训练效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能显著提升大语言模型推理能力，但面临训练不稳定性和多样性崩溃问题。现有优势函数方法繁多，缺乏清晰的选择标准和适用场景，亟需统一框架指导。

Method: 提出统一框架将任意优势函数分解为符号轴和难度轴上的正负梯度质量，分析训练动态中的权衡关系。基于此设计FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy）方法，通过读取训练动态自动调度梯度权重，自适应平衡探索与利用阶段。

Result: FADE在7B规模上比最佳静态基线提前20k步达到峰值pass@1，在32B规模上提前2k步。在LiveCodeBench和AIME数据集上，在所有pass@k指标上均达到最佳准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: FADE通过自适应优势函数调度，有效解决了强化学习后训练中的稳定性和多样性问题，在多个规模上显著加速训练并提升模型推理性能，为LLM推理训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [38] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和泛化误差之间的关系呈现非单调性：在强隐私保护（高噪声）机制下，隐私增强反而降低泛化误差，两者无冲突；而在弱隐私保护（低噪声）机制下，鲁棒性与隐私之间存在权衡，隐私增强会损害泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和优化误差之间存在根本性的三元悖论。本文旨在探究这一三元关系是否同样适用于泛化误差，并揭示隐私保护强度对该关系的影响。

Method: 通过建立LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的算法稳定性下界与上界，从理论上刻画泛化误差的非单调行为，并设计实验进行验证和深入分析。

Result: 证明了泛化误差与隐私保护强度之间存在非单调关系：高噪声机制下隐私增强降低泛化误差，无鲁棒性与隐私的冲突；低噪声机制下鲁棒性与隐私的冲突重现，隐私增强损害泛化性能。理论结果通过匹配的上下界得到严格证明，并经实验验证。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与泛化误差的三元关系并非普适，而是依赖于隐私机制的具体设定，这一发现为分布式学习中的隐私-鲁棒性权衡提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [39] [Towards Learning Representations of Policies in Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01498)
*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了在两人零和不完全信息博弈中学习策略表示（嵌入）的方法，通过构建数据集、提出嵌入学习方法和设计下游评估任务，证明了即使是基础方法也能学习到有用的行为表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两人零和不完全信息博弈中，如何学习有用的策略表示（嵌入）是一个重要但未被充分研究的问题。现有工作缺乏系统性比较自监督学习技术在博弈策略表示学习中的应用。

Conclusion: 即使在基础方法下也能学习到有用的策略表示，为未来研究提供了基准框架。研究代码已开源，便于其他研究者在此基础上进行扩展。

To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

Result: 在100K-1M token范围的长上下文基准测试中，MHM-LRU显著改善了记忆保留率和端到端准确率。在896K token的RULER-HQA任务上，记忆保留率从不30%提升至73.96%。这些改进在不同模型家族、规模和任务类型中均具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下水平风速均方根误差为0.40 m/s，方向误差3.2度，在垂直上升剖面泛化中技能得分为0.861；强化学习控制器在4-12 m/s平均风速下水平轨迹跟踪误差相比风盲PD基线降低48%，在100%评估回合中获胜；消融实验表明风感知贡献随风速增加而增大，在强风中约占总体效益的一半；控制器在13-15 m/s分布外风况下性能优雅退化，而基线则灾难性失效。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段学习框架成功实现了局部风场估计与飞行控制的结合，风估计精度接近未解析湍流所施加的理论下限，控制器在广泛风况范围内显著提升轨迹跟踪性能，且具有良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为大气边界层中的小型多旋翼飞行器控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>

*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认证世界模型的“感知时钟”机制，将有效性视野转化为具体的重感知决策规则，并通过漂移感知方法确保部署后的实际保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证世界模型能够估计预测保持有效的时间长度，但如何将这一有效性视野转化为操作性的感知决策规则（即何时停止滑行并重新感知）尚不明确。现有方法往往高估了滑行有效性，导致实际部署时保证失效。

Method: 从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导无感知间隔的截止期限。提出漂移感知的部署方法：单纯依赖流形上的李雅普诺夫速率会高估滑行有效性，而校准的原生滚动漂移包络能提供可部署的保证。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型上实现该时钟。

Result: 在冻结VN-JEPA模型上，该方法有效控制了跨种子和数据分片的间隔同步证书违规；在定理床测试中，该时钟在部署分布上保持有效，并在匹配感知预算下相比精确混合期望信念调度显著减少了事件尾违规；但在短视界冻结VN-JEPA场景下，经验保形视野与部署时钟在有效性和预算上相当。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出了认证感知时钟原语和漂移感知部署方法，而非声称谱时钟在经验上优于所有非谱调度器；同时明确了方法的局限性。

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>

Method: 提出进化特征工程（EFE）框架，将预处理变换表示为具有标准化fit/transform接口的Python程序，可直接插入现有机器学习流程。在进化过程中，候选程序利用数据集上下文、摘要统计信息和验证集上的下游性能反馈进行迭代优化。框架在两个场景中实现：针对时间序列预测的EFE-Time学习可逆的、数据集特定的归一化方法；针对表格预测的EFE-Tab演化紧凑的特征程序。

Result: 在时间序列预测任务中，EFE-Time在数据集上平均降低3%以上的预测误差（MASE、WQL、MAE），在COVID-Deaths数据集上改进达19%，这些改进在Chronos-2等最新TSFMs上同样有效。在表格预测任务中，EFE-Tab演化出的紧凑特征程序在经典决策树上表现尤为出色，小规模特征集即可实现有竞争力的准确率同时保持可解释性。

Conclusion: EFE框架证明了基于LLM的进化方法能够在自动处理结构化数据时同时提升准确性和可解释性，为自动化特征工程提供了有效解决方案，特别适用于需要保持模型可解释性的应用场景。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as open-ended search operators in evolutionary optimization. We introduce Evolutionary Feature Engineering (EFE), a framework for using LLM-based evolution to discover preprocessing transformations for structured data. EFE represents transformations as Python programs with a standardized fit/transform interface, allowing them to be inserted directly into existing machine learning pipelines. During evolution, candidate programs are refined using dataset context, summary statistics, and downstream performance feedback on validation set. We instantiate EFE in two settings. For time-series forecasting, EFE-Time learns invertible, dataset-specific normalizations that improve off-the-shelf time-series foundation models. It reduces forecasting errors (MASE, WQL, MAE) 3% or more when averaged across datasets and improvements are as much as 19% on the COVID-Deaths dataset. Notably, these improvements occur with recent TSFMs such as Chronos-2. For tabular prediction, EFE-Tab evolves compact feature programs that add useful interpretable features and remove redundant ones, improving or matching existing LLM-based feature-engineering methods. We found EFE-Tab to be particularly effective on classical decision trees, where small sets of evolved features yield competitive accuracy while preserving interpretability. Overall, EFE demonstrates that LLM-based evolution can improve both accuracy and interpretability when automatically tackling structured data.

</details>

*Leyan Li,Rennong Yang,Zhenxing Zhang,Liping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-LogSMask，一种可解释的多头对数结构掩码，通过将对称归一化的图拓扑直接注入注意力逻辑值，使标准Transformer能有效处理图数据，在20个基准测试中13个达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的自注意力机制（全连接）与图数据的特性（稀疏、结构化、多尺度）不匹配。现有图Transformer通过结构编码、混合消息传递或学习注意力约束解决此问题，但增加了额外复杂性且可解释性有限。

Method: 提出X-LogSMask：将对数变换应用于对称归一化的邻接矩阵，将结构连接性转换为拓扑感知的门控信号；不同注意力头分配不同幂次的归一化邻接矩阵，赋予每个头明确的结构半径，支持单层内的多跳信息传播。

Result: 在20个节点级、边级和图级基准测试中，配备X-LogSMask的Transformer在13个数据集上达到SOTA性能，在轻量级单层配置下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单、可解释的结构掩码可以使自注意力成为有效的图学习算子，无需改变Transformer架构，证明了拓扑约束替代无限制自注意力的有效性。

Abstract: Transformers have become general-purpose architectures, but their all-to-all self-attention is poorly matched to graph data, whose interactions are sparse, structured and multi-scale. Existing Graph Transformers address this mismatch through structural encodings, hybrid message-passing modules or learned attention constraints, often introducing additional complexity and limited interpretability. Here we introduce X-LogSMask, an explainable multi-head logarithmic structural mask that injects symmetrically normalized graph topology directly into attention logits. The logarithmic transform converts structural connectivity into a topology-aware gating signal, suppressing unsupported node interactions while preserving feature-dependent attention. By assigning different powers of the normalized adjacency matrix to different attention heads, X-LogSMask gives each head a defined structural radius and supports multi-hop information propagation within a single layer. We further show that a standard Transformer encoder can be interpreted as one-step message passing on a complete graph, motivating X-LogSMask as a topology-constrained alternative to unrestricted self-attention. Across 20 node-, edge- and graph-level benchmarks, Transformers equipped with X-LogSMask achieve state-of-the-art performance on 13 datasets and remain competitive in a lightweight one-layer configuration. These results show that simple, interpretable structural masks can make self-attention an effective graph-learning operator without changing the Transformer architecture. The code is available at https://github.com/LiLeyan-0120/X-LogSMask.

</details>

*Aria Masoomi,Mahsa Bazzaz,Adel Javanmard,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从几何视角研究CoT推理，发现轨迹复杂度能区分任务难度（AUC=0.93），且运动学特征能在生成20%的token时预测答案正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT研究主要关注推理链的长度和内容，对其在模型隐藏状态空间中的内部几何特性知之甚少。理解这些几何特性有助于揭示任务难度和解决方案质量的本质。

Method: 将每个推理链形式化为R^d空间中的离散曲线，引入有效维度d_ρ度量轨迹复杂度，通过谱、位置和运动学几何泛函刻画轨迹特性，并使用这些特征进行任务难度区分和正确性预测。

Result: 在MATH500数据集上，d_ρ在区分易难问题上达到0.93 AUC；运动学特征仅需前20%生成token即可预测解答正确性，且该正确性特征在不同难度问题上具有迁移性。

Conclusion: 模型内部推理轨迹的几何形状是洞察任务难度和解决方案质量的有原则窗口，为未来早期停止策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex problems by generating intermediate reasoning steps. While much attention has been paid to the length and content of these reasoning chains, far less is known about their internal geometry. We study the \emph{geometry} of CoT trajectories in the hidden state space of transformer models, formalizing each reasoning chain as a discrete curve in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and characterizing it through spectral, positional, and kinematic geometric functionals. We introduce the effective dimension $d_ρ$ as a measure of trajectory complexity and show theoretically that trajectories with flatter eigenvalue spectra correspond to harder tasks, as they explore more of the hidden dimensions. Lastly, we explore how kinematic features of the trajectory, mean position, positional dispersion, initial and current hidden states, mean velocity, mean speed, and speed dispersion, can be used to predict solution correctness before generation is complete, and may inform future early-stopping strategies. Experimentally, on mathematical reasoning problems from the MATH500 dataset, $d_ρ$ achieves $0.93$ AUC in distinguishing easy from hard problems, while kinematic features potentially can predict correctness from only the first $20\%$ of generated tokens. These correctness signatures transfer across questions of varying difficulty, establishing that the shape of a model's internal reasoning trajectory is a principled window into both task hardness and solution quality.

</details>


### [47] [BOUNDARY_SYNC: Measuring Communication-Induced Representational Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01600)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，发现LLM智能体间通信会导致输出趋同（同质化），文本和图像通信均产生显著同质化效应（CAF<1），群体规模可调节耦合方向（K=5趋同，K=3可能分化），耦合由提示词驱动而非累积更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被部署为通信智能体，需要理解智能体间通信是否会导致输出收敛（同质化），这对多智能体系统设计具有重要意义。

Method: 提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，定义耦合放大因子CAF=JSD_cond/JSD_baseline（CAF<1表示同质化，CAF>1表示多样化）；使用GPT-4o进行受控实验（N=30，约9,900次API调用），测量文本和图像通信的耦合效应，设置无通信消融和提示扰动对照。

Result: (1)文本通信导致显著同质化（CAF=0.803，d=1.30，p<0.001）；(2)图像通信同样产生同质化（CAF=0.834）；(3)群体规模调节耦合方向——K=5产生同质化，K=3产生CAF>1.0（可能转向多样化）；(4)跨模型复现显示极端变异（CAF 0.034-0.803）；(5)耦合是无状态的——由提示语境驱动而非累积更新。

Conclusion: LLM智能体耦合是真实、可测量且在提示层面可控的，这些发现对多智能体系统设计具有直接意义，为控制智能体群体行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as communicating agents, does inter-agent communication cause outputs to converge? We introduce BOUNDARY_SYNC, a protocol measuring representational coupling via the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline), where CAF < 1 indicates homogenization and CAF > 1 indicates diversification. In controlled GPT-4o experiments (N=30, ~9,900 API calls), we measure coupling in text and image communication. Key findings: (1) text communication causes significant homogenization (CAF=0.803 [0.740, 0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001), confirmed by no-communication ablation and prompt-perturbation controls; (2) image communication also homogenizes under within-modality baselines (CAF=0.834 [0.811, 0.858]), with comparable proportional effect; (3) group size moderates coupling direction -- K=5 produces homogenization while K=3 yields CAF > 1.0 (point estimates 1.14 and 1.06, CI pending), suggesting a directional shift toward diversification; (4) cross-model replication shows extreme variation (CAF 0.034-0.803), with DeepSeek dominated by format artifacts; (5) coupling is stateless -- driven by prompt context rather than cumulative updating, with continuous consensus producing monotonic convergence. These results establish LLM agent coupling as real, measurable, and controllable at the prompt level, with direct implications for multi-agent system design.

</details>


### [48] [SINA: A Fully Automated Circuit Schematic Image to Netlist Generator Using Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01609)
*Saoud Aldowaish,Yashwanth Karumanchi,Kai-Chen Chiang,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Finn Murphy,Rishen Cao,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SINA，一个开源的电路原理图图像到网表生成器，通过集成深度学习、连通分量标记、OCR和视觉语言模型，实现了96.67%的网表生成准确率，是现有最先进方法的2.72倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在电子设计自动化中的应用受限于缺乏机器可读的电路设计知识表示。研究文献、教科书和网站中的电路原理图图像包含了大量经验证的设计，但这些视觉表示无法被EDA工具直接处理。当前的转换方法在IC和PCB级原理图的泛化能力不足，且在元件识别、连接推断以及区分连接点和交叉线方面存在困难。

Method: 提出SINA，一个全自动化的开源流程：1) 使用深度学习进行鲁棒的元件检测；2) 采用连通分量标记进行准确的连接推断；3) 集成光学字符识别(OCR)提取元件参考标识符；4) 利用视觉语言模型(VLM)进行可靠的参考标识符分配。系统能处理IC和PCB级原理图，并包含专门的交叉线检测来区分导线交叉和连接点。

Result: 实验结果表明，SINA的总体网表生成准确率达到96.67%，相比现有最先进方法提高了2.72倍。通过图同构技术验证了生成网表的正确性。

Conclusion: SINA成功解决了电路原理图图像到机器可读网表转换的关键挑战，为AI驱动的电路设计应用提供了重要的基础设施支持，显著推进了模拟和混合信号领域的自动化设计能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have revolutionized Electronic Design Automation (EDA), particularly through Large Language Models (LLMs) for circuit design tasks. However, their application to analog and mixed-signal domains remains limited by the lack of machine-readable representations of existing circuit design knowledge. Circuit schematic images found in research manuscripts, textbooks, and websites constitute a vast repository of validated designs; however, these visual representations cannot be directly processed by EDA tools. Converting them into machine-readable netlists is essential for enabling simulation, verification, and building comprehensive databases for AI-based models. Current conversion methods lack generalization across both Integrated Circuit (IC) and Printed Circuit Board (PCB) level schematics. Moreover, they struggle with component recognition and connectivity inference, and fail to distinguish between connected junctions and crossing wires. In this paper, we propose SINA, an open-source circuit schematic image-to-netlist generator. SINA is a fully automated pipeline that integrates deep learning for robust component detection, connected-component labeling for accurate connectivity inference, Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for component reference designator extraction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reliable reference designator assignment. SINA handles both IC- and PCB-level schematics and incorporates dedicated crossing-wires detection to differentiate wire intersections from connections. We validate the correctness of the generated netlists using graph isomorphism techniques. Our experiments demonstrate an overall netlist generation accuracy of 96.67%, which is 2.72x higher compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [49] [MKGR: Multimodal Knowledge-Graph Representation Learning for Cold-Start Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01627)
*Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为\method的多模态表示框架，用于解决蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用(PPI)预测中的冷启动问题，通过结合区域感知的蛋白质序列编码和四个以蛋白质为中心的生物医学知识图谱，在冷启动设置下显著优于现有的序列、网络和知识图谱基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PPI预测中，当候选相互作用的蛋白质在训练期间没有观察到PPI边时，仅依赖网络拓扑的模型会失去有用的上下文信息，这是一个困难但重要的冷启动问题。准确的PPI预测对于功能基因组学、疾病机制发现和药物开发至关重要。

Method: 提出了\method多模态表示框架：(1)序列分支：从结构信息的序列区域提取上下文表示；(2)图分支：利用图注意力编码器从稀疏生物医学关联中学习模态特定的蛋白质嵌入，整合四个知识图谱（蛋白质-药物、蛋白质-疾病、蛋白质-miRNA、蛋白质-lncRNA）；(3)桥接重建目标：通过恢复共享的蛋白质-实体关联来正则化图学习；(4)配对级门控模块：自适应地整合每个候选蛋白质对的序列和图证据。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，在novel-old和novel-novel两种冷启动设置下，\method在ACC、F1、AUC、AUPR和MCC指标上始终优于竞争性的序列、网络和知识图谱基线方法。

Conclusion: \method成功解决了PPI预测中的冷启动问题，通过多模态融合策略有效结合序列信息和知识图谱信息，证明了该方法在处理未见蛋白质相互作用预测任务中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Accurate protein-protein interaction (PPI) prediction is central to functional genomics, disease mechanism discovery, and drug development. A difficult setting arises when candidate interactions include proteins that have no observed PPI edges during training, where models relying on network topology alone often lose useful context. This paper presents \method, a multimodal representation framework for cold-start PPI prediction. \method\ combines region-aware protein sequence encoding with four protein-centered biomedical knowledge graphs, including protein-drug, protein-disease, protein-miRNA, and protein-lncRNA associations. The sequence branch extracts contextual representations from structurally informed sequence regions, while graph attention encoders learn modality-specific protein embeddings from sparse biomedical associations. A bridge reconstruction objective regularizes graph learning by recovering shared protein-entity associations, and a pair-level gating module adaptively integrates sequence and graph evidence for each candidate protein pair. Experiments on two benchmark datasets under novel-old and novel-novel cold-start settings show that \method\ consistently outperforms competitive sequence, network, and knowledge-graph baselines across ACC, F1, AUC, AUPR, and MCC.

</details>


### [50] [CALM: Interpretable Cross-Modal Alignment for Biomarker Discovery from Unpaired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01656)
*Jueqi Wang,Zachary Jacokes,John Darrell Van Horn,Kevin A. Pelphrey,Michael C. Schatz,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CALM框架，通过在共享潜在空间中对齐神经影像和遗传数据，从完全分离的人群中学习可解释的脑区-遗传通路关联，并在自闭症谱系障碍研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解脑结构与遗传影响的相互作用对于研究神经精神疾病至关重要，但大多数大规模数据集是单模态的，仅提供神经影像或遗传数据之一，缺乏成对的多模态数据，这限制了跨模态关联研究的开展。

Method: 提出CALM框架，通过线性投影将两种模态对齐到共享潜在空间中，同时匹配类条件潜在分布并确保组间可分性；这些投影提供可解释的通路-ROI关联；训练时使用单模态影像和遗传数据集，使模型能够泛化到未见的配对数据集。

Result: CALM在未见配对数据集上的泛化性能优于多种最先进方法和消融基线；学习到的关联相对于配对基线具有稳定性；在自闭症谱系障碍实验中，揭示了与特定皮层区域相关的免疫和代谢通路，结果与已发表文献一致。

Conclusion: CALM能够利用大规模单模态数据仓库研究不同数据集间的跨模态交互，为神经精神疾病的脑-遗传关联研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: The interaction between brain structure and genetic influences is key to understanding neuropsychiatric disorders. However, most large-scale datasets are unimodal, providing either neuroimaging or genetics data. We propose CALM, a framework that learns interpretable associations between brain ROIs and genetic pathways from completely disjoint populations. CALM aligns the two modalities in a shared latent space via linear projections that simultaneously match the class-conditional latent distributions and ensure group separability. These projections provide interpretable pathway--ROI associations. When trained on unimodal imaging and genetics datasets, CALM generalizes to an unseen paired dataset, outperforming several state-of-the-art methods and ablation baselines. We also demonstrate stability of the learned associations against a paired baseline. Our experiments on autism spectrum disorder reveal immune and metabolic pathways linked to specific cortical regions and are consistent with established literature. Thus, CALM opens the door to leveraging large unimodal repositories for studying cross-modal interactions in brain disorders across disparate datasets.

</details>


### [51] [Message Passing Based Two-Timescale Bayesian Learning for Joint Channel and Memory Hardware Impairments Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01660)
*Wei Xu,An Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于消息传递的双时间尺度贝叶斯深度学习(MP-TTBDL)框架，用于解决大规模MIMO接收机中硬件损伤导致的信道估计问题，通过联合跟踪快速变化的信道和缓慢变化的硬件损伤，实现了比传统方法更优的估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO接收机中的硬件损伤会引入符号间记忆和元件间耦合，严重降低信道估计性能。由于无线信道在时隙间快速变化，而硬件损伤因硬件老化和环境变化而缓慢漂移，这种不同的物理时间尺度特性需要专门的建模和估计方法。

Method: 采用残差循环门控单元(RGRU)建模硬件损伤的时隙内记忆，构建多时隙因子图。为快速变化的稀疏信道分配快变马尔可夫先验，为慢变的网络参数分配慢变高斯马尔可夫先验。开发消息传递算法，将时隙内因子图分解为信道跟踪模块和损伤校准模块：前者通过Turbo-OAMP进行稀疏信道估计，后者通过深度近似消息传递(DAMP)更新损伤参数，两模块通过期望传播(EP)迭代交换外信息直至收敛。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架在不同在线损伤场景和信噪比条件下，能够稳健地实现比传统补偿器后接信道估计更低的信道估计误差。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MP-TTBDL框架成功捕获了信道和硬件损伤的不同时间尺度特性，通过联合跟踪和校准机制有效缓解了硬件损伤对信道估计的影响，展现了良好的鲁棒性和估计精度优势。

Abstract: Hardware impairments in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) receivers introduce inter-symbol memory and inter-element coupling, severely degrading channel estimation. This paper employs a residual recurrent gated unit (RGRU) to model the intra-slot memory of the hardware impairments and proposes a message-passing-based two-timescale Bayesian deep learning (MP-TTBDL) framework for joint channel and impairment tracking. Owing to small-scale fading, the wireless channel varies rapidly across slots, whereas hardware impairments drift slowly due to hardware aging and environmental variations. To capture these distinct physical timescales, a fastvarying Markov prior and a slow-varying Gaussian Markov prior are assigned to the sparse channel and the network parameters, respectively. Based on a multi-slot factor graph formulation, a message-passing algorithm is developed. Specifically, the inter-slot messages admit closed-form updates, while the intra-slot factor graph, due to its complex recurrent structure, is partitioned into a channel tracking module and an impairments calibration module. The channel tracking module performs sparse channel estimation via turbo orthogonal approximate message passing (Turbo-OAMP), and the impairments calibration module updates the impairment parameters via a specially designed deep approximate message passing (DAMP) procedure, with the two modules iteratively exchanging extrinsic information through expectation propagation (EP) until convergence. Simulation results show that the proposed framework robustly achieves lower channel estimation error than conventional compensators followed by channel estimation across different online impairment scenarios and signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions.

</details>


### [52] [Revisiting Decentralized Online Convex Optimization with Compressed Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01665)
*Hao Zhou,Xiaoyu Wang,Chang Yao,Mingli Song,Yuanyu Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次提出了两种适用于压缩通信去中心化在线凸优化的FTRL型算法，在全信息设定下匹配现有结果，在赌博机设定下显著改进了遗憾界限和通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化在线凸优化压缩通信算法均为OGD变体，而在精确通信场景下FTRL变体表现更优。为了填补这一空白并利用FTRL的优势，研究D-OCO压缩通信场景下的FTRL型算法。

Method: 提出两种FTRL型算法：1）全信息设定下的算法；2）赌博机设定下的算法。核心创新是利用FTRL的双重更新机制，使得平均共识与通信压缩技术能够简单结合。

Result: 全信息设定下的算法能够匹配现有的遗憾界限；赌博机设定下的算法显著改进了现有的遗憾界限和通信成本。

Conclusion: FTRL型算法在D-OCO压缩通信场景下不仅在算法设计和理论分析上更为优雅，还能取得优于传统OGD型算法的性能表现，为该领域提供了新的算法设计思路。

Abstract: Decentralized online convex optimization (D-OCO) is a popular framework for distributed applications with streaming data. To tackle the communication bottleneck, previous studies have investigated D-OCO with compressed communication and proposed several algorithms that are variants of online gradient descent (OGD). However, for D-OCO with exact communication, the best existing algorithms are variants of follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In this paper, for the first time, we propose two FTRL-type algorithms for D-OCO with compressed communication. Compared with OGD-type algorithms, our algorithms are more elegant in both algorithmic design and theoretical analysis. The key insight is that the dual update mechanism of FTRL allows us to make a simple application of the technique for average consensus with communication compression. More specifically, our first algorithm considers the full-information setting, and can match the existing regret bounds. Our second algorithm is designed for the bandit setting, and can significantly improve both the regret bounds and communication costs of existing algorithms.

</details>


### [53] [UniWind: Toward Unified Day-Ahead Wind Power Forecasting via Physics-Informed State Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01670)
*Ronghui Xu,Tongxin Wu,Guozhen Zhang,Yihan Li,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniWind，一种基于物理先验状态路由的风电功率预测模型，通过结合物理约束和数据驱动方法，有效解决了现有模型难以区分气象效应与运行状态偏差的问题，在20多个真实数据集上展现了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前风电功率预测对电力系统经济运行至关重要。现有方法存在关键问题：(1)实际风电场观测功率交织了物理可用功率、局部环境效应和潜在运行状态（如停机、限电）；(2)物理模型提供有用约束但跨风电场适应性差；(3)数据驱动模型能捕获丰富相关性但常混淆气象效应与状态诱导的偏差。亟需一种能同时利用物理约束和数据驱动优势的方法。

Method: 提出UniWind模型，包含三个核心组件：(1)物理先验估计器：通过结合站点条件单调变形和共享物理功率曲线，构建站点校准的物理先验，并应用物理上界约束将其塑造为可用风电功率的软包络；(2)潜在状态编码器：建模运行状态嵌入；(3)状态感知功率校正器：通过知识引导的监督状态路由和有界的状态特定专家校正，将物理先验转化为最终功率预测。

Result: 在超过20个真实世界数据集上进行了全样本和跨风电场零样本实验，验证了UniWind模型的准确性和鲁棒性。实验结果表明该方法能有效处理不同风电场的预测任务。

Conclusion: UniWind成功融合了物理先验知识与数据驱动方法，通过物理上界约束和状态路由机制，有效区分了气象效应与运行状态偏差，为风电功率预测提供了一个准确、鲁棒且具有良好泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Day-ahead wind power forecasting is essential for cost-effective power-system operation. It is primarily driven by future meteorological conditions while retaining temporal dependencies in power generation. In practice, observed wind-farm power often entangles physically available power with local environmental effects and latent operational states, such as shutdowns and curtailment. Existing physical models provide useful constraints but adapt poorly across wind farms, whereas data-driven models can capture rich correlations but often conflate meteorological effects with state-induced deviations. In this study, we propose UniWind, a wind power forecasting model based on physics-informed state routing. UniWind first employs a Physical Prior Estimator to construct a site-calibrated physical prior by combining site-conditioned monotonic warping with a shared physical power curve. It further applies a physical upper-bound constraint to shape this prior as a soft envelope of available wind power generation. UniWind then proposes a Latent State Encoder to model operating-state embeddings and transforms the physical prior into final power forecasts through a State-aware Power Corrector, which uses knowledge-guided supervised state routing and bounded, state-specific expert correction. Full-shot and cross-farm zero-shot experiments on more than 20 real-world datasets demonstrate the accuracy and robustness of UniWind.

</details>


### [54] [WARP: Weight-Space Analysis for Recovering Training Data Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01686)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Aditya Goyal,John Cooper,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WARP框架，能够仅从公开发布的模型权重中恢复出微调模型的训练数据混合比例，解决了基础模型训练数据配方不公开的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型公开发布时很少披露其训练数据配方（如领域混合权重），导致研究人员无法了解产生这些模型的训练分布，形成了访问不对称。现有的成员推断等方法只能在个体样本层面检测，无法刻画训练语料库的全局组成。

Method: WARP通过模型合并技术，在基础模型和微调模型之间进行插值，生成伪检查点来近似缺失的训练轨迹，并在权重空间中暴露训练数据的几何足迹。从这些模拟足迹中提取几何特征，使用无参数的softmax读取函数或在合成混合数据上训练的MLP投影器，将特征映射到领域比例。

Result: 在BERT和GPT-2的受控实验中，WARP恢复领域混合比例的平均MAE分别低至0.046和0.104，性能优于成员推断方法以及能够访问真实训练轨迹的变体方法。

Conclusion: WARP成功实现了从模型权重中恢复训练数据混合比例，为解决基础模型训练数据不透明问题提供了有效途径，有助于提升AI模型的透明度和可解释性。

Abstract: Foundation models are routinely released to the public, yet the data recipes used to train them -- such as domain mixture weights that determine how different sources are sampled -- are rarely disclosed. This creates an access asymmetry: researchers study the resulting models but lack visibility into the training distribution that produces them. Prior works for inferring training data, such as membership inference, detect at the level of individual samples and thus cannot characterize the global composition of the training corpus. We introduce WARP, a framework that recovers a fine-tuned model's training mixtures directly from its released weights. WARP interpolates between the base and fine-tuned models using model merging, generating pseudo-checkpoints that approximate the missing training trajectory and expose a geometric footprint of the training data in the weight space. From these simulated footprints, WARP extracts geometric features and maps them to domain proportions using either a parameter-free softmax readout or an MLP projector trained on synthetic mixtures. In controlled experiments with BERT and GPT-2, WARP recovers domain mixtures with an average MAE as low as 0.046 and 0.104 respectively, outperforming membership inference and a variant with access to the true training trajectory.

</details>


### [55] [Model Merging as Probabilistic Inference in Fine-Tuning Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01689)
*Long Minh Bui,Tuan Anh Le Van,Tung Phi Duc,Phi Le Nguyen,Jana Doppa,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于乘积专家(PoE)和能量模型(EBM)的模型合并新框架，通过使用重尾柯西专家替代高斯假设来更好地捕捉模型残差的统计特性，在多个任务和架构上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法主要基于局部解空间的几何特性，但几何视角在评估任务特定更新方向在合并过程中的统计有用性方面提供有限指导。此外，现有方法隐含地假设方向残差服从轻尾高斯分布，但实证发现这些残差通常呈现重尾特性，导致与假设的不匹配。

Method: 将模型合并问题形式化为乘积专家(PoE)场景下的概率推断问题，每个单任务解定义一个关于合并参数的基于能量的专家模型(EBM)。针对观察到的重尾残差特性，提出了基于柯西专家的重尾PoE设计，替代传统的高斯假设，并提供了可证明收敛的推断过程。

Result: 实验表明，现有多种模型合并方法可视为该框架在隐含高斯假设下的特例。所提出的重尾柯西专家设计能更好地捕捉观察到的残差行为。在多个任务和架构上的实验显示，该方法相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 从概率推断视角重新构建模型合并问题提供了一个统一的理论框架，能够解释现有方法并识别其局限性。通过采用更符合实际残差分布的重尾模型，可以有效提升模型合并的性能。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine existing single-task solutions into a multi-task solution without additional data-driven fine-tuning.~Most existing approaches achieve this using geometric properties of local solution spaces. However, such geometric views provide limited guidance for scoring how statistically useful each task-specific update direction is across tasks during merging. To address this, we formulate model merging from a new perspective of probabilistic inference under a product-of-experts (PoE) scenario where each single-task solution defines an energy-based expert model (EBM) over the merged parameters. We show that several existing model merging methods arise as special cases of our framework under energy designs that impose implicit Gaussian assumptions on directional residuals between merged and task-specific models. Empirically, we find that these residuals are often heavy-tailed which exposes a mismatch with the imposed light-tailed Gaussian structures. We address this with a heavy-tailed PoE design based on Cauchy experts, which better captures the observed residual behavior while admitting a provably convergent inference procedure. Experiments across multiple tasks and architectures show significant improvements over state-of-the-arts baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MinhLong210/PoE-EBM-Merging.git.

</details>

*Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向概率背景研究生的扩散模型导论，从经典采样动力学出发，系统地介绍现代扩散采样器、误差分析及推理时控制，采用分层结构提供完整证明、简化假设下的估计及研究级定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为概率背景的研究生提供扩散模型的证明导向入门教程，填补缺乏随机微分方程、随机数值方法或扩散模型先验知识的学生与该领域之间的知识鸿沟。

Method: 采用分层教学方法：核心定义和恒等式给出完整证明；代表性估计在简化假设下证明；研究级定理给出证明路线图。从经典采样动力学出发，追溯至现代扩散采样器的发展脉络。

Result: 构建了从经典采样动力学到现代扩散采样器的完整理论框架，包含误差分析和推理时控制的系统介绍，并按难度分层呈现证明内容。

Conclusion: 为初学者提供了扩散模型的理论入门，通过证明导向的方式建立了从经典采样到现代扩散方法的桥梁，适合作为该领域的研究起点。

Abstract: These notes give a proof-oriented introduction to diffusion models from the viewpoint of sampling, tracing a single arc from classical sampling dynamics to modern diffusion samplers, their error analysis, and inference-time control. Throughout, the material is layered into core definitions and identities proved in full, representative estimates proved under simplifying assumptions, and research-level theorems stated with a proof roadmap. The intended audience is beginning graduate students with a background in probability but no prior exposure to stochastic differential equations, stochastic numerics, or diffusion models.

</details>


### [57] [Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine for Solving High-Frequency Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01694)
*Xiong Xiong,Ruonan Zhai,Zheng Zeng,Sheng Zhou,Rongchun Hu,Zichen Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频率偏移物理信息极限学习机（FS-PIELM）框架，通过平移高斯权重分布均值的方式解决神经网络的谱偏差问题，在求解高频偏微分方程时相比现有方法提升1至近5个数量级的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息机器学习在求解具有高频解的偏微分方程时面临谱偏差挑战，即神经网络倾向于优先学习低频分量，难以捕捉高频解。现有基于缩放因子的权重初始化方法会导致方差放大问题。

Method: 提出频率偏移物理信息极限学习机（FS-PIELM）框架，采用加性机制进行权重初始化：通过平移高斯权重分布的均值而非乘以缩放因子来调整频率，同时保持方差固定为1。开发了两种变体：FS-PIELM-L为每个神经元分配独立的频率幅值，FS-PIELM-G将神经元分组以提高鲁棒性。该方法保持极限学习机的计算效率，仅需一次线性求解。

Result: 在涵盖Helmholtz、波动、Poisson、Klein-Gordon、热传导和对流扩散六类方程的七个基准问题上进行实验，包括规则和复杂几何区域。线性变体在七个案例中的六个取得了最佳精度，相比现有PIELM变体提升1至近5个数量级。理论分析表明频率方差保持有界且趋近于1，与传统方法的二次增长形成对比。

Conclusion: FS-PIELM通过频率偏移机制有效解决了谱偏差问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了高频PDE求解精度，为物理信息机器学习领域提供了一种有效的权重初始化策略。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency solutions remains a central challenge in physics-informed machine learning due to spectral bias -- the tendency of neural networks to learn low-frequency components preferentially. This paper proposes a Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine (FS-PIELM) framework that addresses this limitation through an additive mechanism for weight initialization. Rather than multiplying random weights by a scaling factor, the method translates the mean of the Gaussian weight distribution while keeping the variance fixed at unity, thereby avoiding the variance amplification inherent in scaling-based methods. Two variants are developed: FS-PIELM-L assigns independent frequency magnitudes to individual neurons, while FS-PIELM-G groups neurons for improved robustness. Theoretical analysis shows that the frequency variance under the proposed framework remains bounded and approaches unity regardless of target frequency, in contrast to the quadratic growth of conventional approaches. The method preserves the computational efficiency of extreme learning machines, requiring only a single linear solve. Experiments on seven benchmark problems spanning six equation types -- Helmholtz, wave, Poisson, Klein-Gordon, heat, and advection-diffusion -- on both regular and complex geometries show that the linear variant achieves the best accuracy in six of seven cases, with improvements of one to nearly five orders of magnitude over existing PIELM variants. The code and data accompanying this manuscript will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xgxgnpu/Physics-informed-vibe-coding/tree/main/FS-PIELM.

</details>


### [58] [Predicting Closed-Loop Performance of Latent World Models: Offline Checkpoint Selection for MPC and Model-Based RL Under Non-Markovian Rewards in LunarLander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01736)
*Nikolai Smolyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的离线检查点选择指标CROF，用于解决世界模型训练中验证损失持续改善但闭环性能已崩溃的问题，在LunarLander v3上实现了优于模型无关基线24.5分的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型训练中选择正确的检查点非常困难：传统的验证损失和多步预测RMSE在闭环性能崩溃后仍持续改善，导致无法准确评估模型质量。

Method: 从最优控制理论中提取结构化验证时诊断方法，在Gymnasium的LunarLander v3上训练RSSM世界模型，以CEM-MPC回报作为闭环质量的oracle，评估40个指标，发现奖励可观测性分数(ROF)是最强预测器，将其与三个结构正则化器结合形成复合奖励可观测性分数(CROF)。

Result: CROF选择的世界模型训练的基于模型A2C策略比公平评估的模型无关A2C基线高约24.5回报分，同时使用约65倍更少的真实环境交互，该世界模型还能驱动强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Conclusion: 提出的CROF指标能够有效解决世界模型检查点选择问题，实现了更高效且性能更优的模型强化学习，为世界模型的验证和选择提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. Code and data: https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM.

</details>


### [59] [Finite-Lag Operator Geometry of Recurrent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01746)
*Kanishka Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了有限滞后算子几何框架，用于分析循环神经网络中的隐藏状态动力学，通过源-后继对(X_t,X_{t+Δ})捕捉瞬态转移模式，揭示了传统静态快照几何分析无法检测到的确定性循环运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环表示几何分析方法通常基于静态快照，忽略了时间演化的动态特性。由于循环表示本质上是轨迹，需要开发能够捕捉有限时间滞后转移模式的分析工具，以理解循环神经网络隐藏状态的时序动态行为。

Method: 提出条件传输律Q_Δ(dy|x)作为核心原语，通过高斯源平滑算子进行估计；推导源中心传输张量G_Δ（可分解为条件扩散和相干位移）和反对称坐标环流W_Δ^ρ；建立线性-高斯闭式解，根据更新矩阵A_Δ、源协方差和创新协方差进行校准。

Result: 证明了仿射协变性、度量依赖性的显式表达、有界轨迹云上的估计器稳定性；有限滞后分离结果显示源中心传输能检测无限小carre-du-champ几何无法记录的确定性循环运动；在重复复制网络实验中揭示了架构依赖的传输尺度差异和相干位移轨迹差异，证实了分解、环流、协变性和稳定性预测。

Conclusion: 有限滞后算子几何框架成功将循环表示的瞬态动力学转化为可计算的几何量，为循环神经网络的动力学分析提供了新的理论工具和定量指标，能够揭示网络架构对隐藏状态演化的深层影响。

Abstract: Recurrent representations are trajectories, but representation geometry is often measured from static snapshots. We develop finite-lag operator geometry for recurrent hidden states from observed source-successor pairs $(X_t,X_{t+Δ})$. The primitive is the conditional transport law $Q_Δ(dy\mid x)$, estimated by a dense Gaussian source-smoothing operator. From this directed finite-lag law we derive a source-centered transport tensor $G_Δ$, which decomposes exactly into conditional spread and coherent displacement, and an antisymmetric coordinate circulation $W_Δ^ρ$, which summarizes directed lagged flow. We prove affine covariance with explicit metric dependence of scalar summaries, dense estimator stability on bounded trajectory clouds, and a finite-lag separation result showing that source-centered transport detects deterministic recurrent motion not recorded by infinitesimal carre-du-champ geometry. A linear-Gaussian closed form calibrates the quantities in terms of the update $A_Δ$, source covariance, and innovation covariance. Controlled experiments validate the decomposition, circulation, covariance, and stability predictions. In performance matched repeat-copy networks, the framework reveals architecture dependent differences in total transport scale and coherent displacement trace, while coherent displacement fraction is metric and resolution dependent.

</details>


### [60] [Efficient Temporal Point Processes via Monotone Alternating Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01752)
*Cheng Wan,Quyu Kong,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单调交替样条（MAS）框架，通过分离插值和外推组件解决单调神经网络（MNN）在TPP累积条件强度函数建模中的三大结构性瓶颈，实现更高拟合精度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单调神经网络（MNN）的累积条件强度函数参数化方法存在三大结构性死锁：凸性限制、饱和极限、以及对CCIF建模要求的违反，这些从根本上限制了模型对复杂时序动态的表示能力。

Method: 提出单调交替样条（MAS）框架，利用独特的插值和外推组件来灵活高效地建模CCIF。插值部分提供强拟合精度，外推部分支持鲁棒泛化，从而减少MNN的不可约近似差距。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，MAS方法在性能上优于现有方法，取得了优越的预测效果。

Conclusion: MAS框架成功解决了MNN的结构性瓶颈问题，为TPP的CCIF建模提供了一个灵活高效的解决方案，在拟合精度和泛化能力方面均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) have widespread applications across various domains. Compared to modeling the conditional intensity of a TPP, modeling its cumulative conditional intensity function (CCIF) improves computational efficiency and eliminates numerical approximation errors. However, current CCIF parameterizations uniformly rely on Monotone Neural Networks (MNNs), which we identify as suffering from three structural deadlocks--convexity restrictions, saturation limits, and violations of CCIF modeling requirements--that fundamentally restrict their representational capacity for complex temporal dynamics. To resolve these bottlenecks, this paper proposes a novel framework called Monotone Alternating Splines (MAS). By leveraging distinct interpolation and extrapolation components, MAS provides a flexible and efficient framework for modeling CCIFs. Theoretically, MAS's interpolation provides strong fitting accuracy, while its extrapolation supports robust generalization, reducing the irreducible approximation gaps of MNNs. Extensive experiments show that MAS achieves superior performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [61] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色感知的神经凸散度头，用于非对称表示学习，通过源/目标角色投影和输入凸Bregman散度来建模有向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多表示学习问题涉及有向关系（如词汇蕴涵、句子蕴涵、本体层次结构和引用链接），但标准的欧氏距离、余弦距离和马氏距离头都是对称的，无法捕捉方向性；通用神经评分器虽然可以建模方向性，但缺乏几何结构。

Method: 提出角色感知神经凸散度头：首先对源和目标角色进行投影，然后评估输入凸神经Bregman散度，在角色投影空间中产生非负结构化分数。该方法具有投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解和基于Hessian的局部曲率等特性。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图基准测试中，角色感知投影在十个随机种子上始终比普通ICNN-Bregman头提高方向准确性，同时保持零观测负散度率。但在大规模固定特征引用预测任务中，专用对称或双曲基线在排序准确性上仍然更强。

Conclusion: 所提出的头最好被理解为一个结构化且可解释的插件式距离模块，适用于方向性关系重要的任务，但在某些大规模固定特征场景下，传统方法仍有优势。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [62] [Denser $\neq$ Better: Limits of On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01763)
*Meng Wang,Haohan Zhao,Wenzhuo Liu,Lu Yang,Geng Liu,Haiyang Guo,Guo-Sen Xie,Gaofeng Meng,Hongbin Liu,Fei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了持续后训练中在线自蒸馏（SDPO）的作用，发现其在领域内专业化时表现良好，但在分布外场景下泛化能力不足，且在持续学习中会导致更严重的遗忘甚至崩溃。相比之下，GRPO等在线强化学习方法表现更为稳健。研究表明，单纯的在线数据不足以支撑持续学习，自蒸馏不应被默认视为持续后训练的稳定器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明在线学习可以缓解遗忘问题，其中在线自蒸馏被认为是一种有前景的方法。然而，作者对这种乐观观点提出质疑，希望深入探究自蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）在持续后训练中的实际表现，特别是其对遗忘问题和泛化能力的影响，从而为持续学习策略的选择提供更清晰的指导。

Method: 作者提出了自蒸馏策略优化（SDPO）方法，并通过系统的实验对比分析其与GRPO等在线强化学习方法在持续后训练场景下的表现。研究从多个维度进行评估：领域内专业化能力、分布外泛化能力、遗忘程度、参数空间和响应空间的漂移程度，以及高频格式伪影的放大效应。同时，作者还分析了教师信号稳定性和对齐程度对自蒸馏效果的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）当教师信号稳定且对齐良好时，SDPO可以加速领域内专业化；（2）SDPO在分布外场景下泛化能力较差；（3）在持续后训练中，SDPO表现出更强的遗忘，甚至可能出现崩溃现象；（4）相比之下，GRPO等在线强化学习方法适应更为保守，能更好地保持先前能力；（5）更密集的自蒸馏会导致参数空间和响应空间更大的漂移；（6）自蒸馏会通过师生自强化循环放大高频格式伪影。

Conclusion: 仅依靠在线数据不足以支撑持续学习。当教师目标稳定且token级别监督可靠时，密集自蒸馏可以加速专业化，但它不应被视为持续后训练的默认稳定器。研究为持续后训练中方法选择提供了重要指导，强调了不同场景下需要谨慎选择合适的学习策略。

Abstract: Continual post-training enables foundation models to acquire new knowledge while preserving existing capabilities. Recent work suggests that on-policy learning can mitigate forgetting, with on-policy self-distillation emerging as a particularly attractive approach. In this work, we revisit this optimistic view through self-distillation policy optimization (SDPO). Our experiments show that SDPO can accelerate in-domain specialization when teacher signals are stable and well aligned, but it struggles to generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios. In continual post-training, SDPO exhibits stronger forgetting and can even collapse, whereas on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO adapt more conservatively and better preserve prior capabilities. Further analyses reveal that denser self-distillation induces larger drift in both parameter space and response space, and can amplify high-frequency formatting artifacts through a self-reinforcing teacher--student loop. These findings suggest that on-policy data alone is insufficient for continual learning. Dense self-distillation can accelerate specialization when teacher targets are stable and token-level supervision is reliable, but it should not be treated as a default stabilizer for continual post-training. Our code is available at https://github.com/Moenupa/SDPO-CL.

</details>


### [63] [Set Diffusion: Interpolating Token Orderings Between Autoregression and Diffusion for Fast and Flexible Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01775)
*Marianne Arriola,Volodymyr Kuleshov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了集扩散（Set Diffusion）语言模型，通过在灵活位置和长度的词元集上进行似然参数化，并配合集因果扩散架构支持KV缓存，实现了任意顺序解码和更优的速度-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型受限于固定长度生成且不支持键值（KV）缓存。块扩散虽部分桥接了扩散与自回归模型，但其固定大小的顺序块限制了解码灵活性和并行性。

Method: 提出集扩散语言模型，包含两个核心组件：(i) 在灵活位置、灵活长度词元集上分解的似然参数化；(ii) 支持每步推理后KV缓存更新的集因果扩散架构。通过在词元集而非固定大小块上分解，支持任意顺序的词元集解码。

Result: 在数学推理、摘要生成和无条件生成任务上，相比先前的扩散语言模型实现了更优的速度-质量权衡；在填充任务上相比块扩散表现出更强性能。

Conclusion: 集扩散成功桥接了扩散模型与自回归模型的优势，通过灵活的词元集分解和KV缓存支持，实现了更高效的推理和更好的生成质量。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have steadily improved in quality relative to autoregressive (AR) models. However, these models are normally constrained to fixed-length generation and do not support key-value (KV) caching. Block diffusion partially bridges diffusion and AR by generating token blocks left-to-right, but its fixed-size sequential blocks limit decoding flexibility and parallelism. Here, we present a new class of language models, set diffusion, comprised of (i) a likelihood parameterization that factorizes over flexible-position, flexible-length token sets and (ii) a set-causal diffusion architecture that supports KV cache updates after every inference step. By factorizing over token sets instead of fixed-size blocks, tokens can be decoded in arbitrarily-ordered sets, including sliding-window sets, enabling faster inference and support for any-order decoding. Set diffusion achieves better speed-quality tradeoffs on mathematical reasoning, summarization, and unconditional generation compared to prior diffusion language models while offering stronger infilling performance than block diffusion. We provide the code, along with the model weights and blog post on the project page: https://m-arriola.com/setdlms/

</details>


### [64] [EHHN: An Event-driven Heterogeneous Hypergraph Network for Object-Centric Next Activity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01785)
*Jiaxing Wang,Kaitao Chen,Zhubin Han,Chenyu Hou,Bin Cao,Jing Fan,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EHHN模型用于对象中心事件日志的下一活动预测，采用异质超图表示和双流架构，在准确率和宏F1上分别最高提升8.1和12.4个百分点，GPU显存占用最高降低24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一活动预测方法假设传统单案例事件日志，而真实服务流程常涉及多类型业务对象共享的事件。对象中心事件日志(OCEL)可捕获此类交互，但现有预测方法存在局限：扁平化方法丢失跨对象上下文，原生OCEL图方法仅通过成对关系编码多对象事件，且现有模型无法联合捕获事件驱动的对象状态变化、事件间时序和全局执行模式。

Method: 提出EHHN(事件驱动异质超图网络)：1) 将每个预测前缀表示为异质超图，其中事件-对象超边绑定共参与对象，生命周期超边分组主对象的生命周期事件；2) 采用双流架构，微空间流建模事件驱动的对象状态演化，宏演化流使用检索的全局原型捕获时序动态；3) 融合双流预测下一活动。

Result: 在四个公开OCEL基准数据集上对比九个基线模型，EHHN在所有数据集上均取得最佳准确率和宏F1分数，相比最强基线分别提升最高8.1和12.4个百分点；相比最强OCEL原生图基线，GPU峰值显存最高降低24倍。

Conclusion: EHHN通过异质超图表示和双流架构有效解决了对象中心事件日志中多对象交互建模、状态演化与时序动态联合捕获的挑战，在预测性能和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法，为服务导向流程的下一活动预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Next activity prediction helps service-oriented processes anticipate upcoming steps before delays, exceptions, or service-level risks occur. Most existing methods assume classical single-case event logs, whereas real service processes often involve events shared by multiple typed business objects. Object-centric event logs (OCELs) capture such interactions, but current predictors remain limited. Flattening-based approaches lose cross-object context, and native OCEL graph-based approaches encode multi-object events through pairwise relations. Existing models also do not jointly capture event-driven object state changes, inter-event timing, and global execution patterns. We propose EHHN, an Event-driven Heterogeneous Hypergraph Network for object-centric next activity prediction. EHHN represents each prediction prefix as a heterogeneous hypergraph, where event--object hyperedges bind retained co-participating objects and a lifecycle hyperedge groups the primary object's observed lifecycle events. Based on this representation, EHHN uses a dual-stream architecture in which a micro-spatial stream models event-driven object-state evolution and a macro-evolution stream captures temporal dynamics using retrieved global prototypes. The two streams are fused to predict the next activity. Experiments on four public OCEL benchmarks against nine baselines show that EHHN achieves the best accuracy and macro F1-score on all datasets, with improvements of up to 8.1 and 12.4 percentage points over the strongest baselines. Compared with the strongest OCEL-native graph baseline, EHHN also reduces peak GPU memory by up to 24 times. Code is available at https://github.com/chenkaitao1112/EHHN.

</details>


### [65] [EPnG: Adaptive Expert Prune-and-Grow for Parameter-Efficient MoE Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01789)
*Ahin Lee,Sehyun Yun,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EPnG框架，通过基于专家重要性的LoRA容量重新分配（修剪低利用率专家、扩展高重要性专家），在固定参数预算下显著提升了MoE模型的微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法如LoRA忽略了MoE的路由动态，导致专家冗余和参数分配不当，造成资源浪费且性能次优。

Method: 提出了EPnG（adaptive prune-and-grow）框架：利用路由器门控概率推导专家重要性，修剪低利用率专家，并通过正交初始化的秩增长扩展高重要性专家，在固定参数预算下重新分配LoRA容量。

Result: 在OLMoE和Qwen1.5-MoE模型上，EPnG在相同预算下持续优于LoRA，性能媲美全量微调，而仅更新0.55%-0.72%的参数（减少140x-180x）。

Conclusion: 将PEFT与MoE路由机制对齐能实现更有效且可扩展的微调策略，为MoE模型的高效适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale efficiently but remain costly to adapt due to redundant experts and uniform parameter allocation. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as LoRA ignore MoE routing dynamics, leading to suboptimal resource use. We propose EPnG, an adaptive prune-and-grow framework that reallocates LoRA capacity based on expert importance derived from router gate probabilities. EPnG prunes under-utilized experts and expands high-importance experts via rank growth with orthogonal initialization, while maintaining a fixed parameter budget. Across OLMoE and Qwen1.5-MoE, EPnG consistently outperforms LoRA under the same budget and achieves performance comparable to full fine-tuning while updating only 0.55%-0.72% of parameters (up to 140x-180x fewer). These results demonstrate that aligning PEFT with MoE routing yields a more effective and scalable fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [66] [Single-Channel EEG-Based Cognitive Load Assessment in Online Learning: A Hybrid Deep Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01795)
*Rowan Hussein,Mohamed Ouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用消费级单通道EEG设备区分在线教育视频内容的难易程度，通过CNN+LSTM+Attention混合模型在受试内评估中达到78.5%准确率（传统方法仅55%），但强调需采用受试无关评估标准，并发布了开源工具供教育者可视化认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习环境中监测认知负荷可帮助教师识别学习者感到困难的内容，但远程学习环境缺失了课堂中支持判断的视觉线索，因此需要探索使用便携式EEG设备作为替代方案。

Method: 使用NeuroSky MindWave Mobile 2单通道消费级EEG设备，采用公开数据集（9名学习者），构建CNN+LSTM+Attention混合模型，结合原始波形和频带功率特征，并使用dropout和L2正则化解决过拟合问题。

Result: 受试内评估中模型准确率达78.5%，相比传统基于特征的分类器（55%）显著提升；正则化将验证准确率稳定在68-73%，消除了训练与验证准确率间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 鉴于样本量小（9名受试者），受试内评估过于乐观；主张受试无关评估（训练和测试数据中无相同学习者）应成为该任务的标准。研究作为可行性研究而非可部署系统，发布了可复现的评估流程和开源notebook工具，可实时记录EEG、运行推理并将认知负荷以热力图形式可视化于视频时间轴上。

Abstract: Monitoring cognitive load during online learning could help instructors identify content that learners find difficult, but remote settings remove the visual cues that support this judgement in a classroom. We study whether a single-channel, consumer-grade EEG device (the NeuroSky MindWave Mobile 2) can distinguish easy from difficult educational-video content, using the publicly available dataset of Wang et al. [24] (ten learners, one excluded for excessive noise, leaving nine). We implement a hybrid CNN+LSTM+Attention model that combines the raw waveform with band-power features. In a within-subject setting, the model reaches up to 78.5% accuracy, compared with 55% for conventional feature-based classifiers; regularization (dropout and L2) closes the large gap between training and validation accuracy that we observe without it, keeping validation accuracy stable at roughly 68-73%. We are deliberately cautious about these numbers: with only nine subjects, within-subject evaluation is optimistic, and we argue that subject-independent evaluation -- in which no learner appears in both training and test data -- should be the standard for this task. To that end we release a reproducible evaluation pipeline. We frame the work as a feasibility study rather than a deployable system, and pair it with an open, notebook-based tool that records EEG, runs inference, and visualizes estimated cognitive load as a heatmap over the video timeline to help educators locate potentially challenging segments.

</details>


### [67] [Expander Sparse Autoencoders: Parameter-Efficient Dictionaries for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01799)
*Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Expander SAEs，通过在TopK SAEs上引入左d-正则扩展图掩码，在保持稀疏编码性能的同时大幅降低解码器参数量和存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏自编码器(SAEs)的密集解码器需要O(mn)个学习参数，在大特征数量时计算和存储成本高昂。作者受压缩感知启发，希望在不牺牲稀疏编码性能的前提下，通过引入结构化的稀疏解码器来降低参数量和计算复杂度。

Method: 提出Expander SAEs：在TopK SAEs架构上，使用左d-正则扩展图掩码约束解码器和绑定的编码器。这种结构使解码器仅需学习dn个参数(d<<m)，同时将OMP算法中的匹配追踪相关步骤转化为O(dn)的聚集-归约操作。作者还提供了理论分析，证明扩展性和列平坦性足以实现无噪k-稀疏码的可辨识性，并推导了OMP精确恢复支撑集的充分条件。

Result: 在Pythia-70M/160M、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-1B上的实验表明：参数d的变化描绘出一致的存储-保真度前沿曲线。在最压缩的现代LM设置下(Qwen2.5-3B，d=7)，相比全密集解码器减少293倍解码器参数，同时保留84%的密集CE损失恢复率。控制实验揭示存储-保真度权衡的改善源于稀疏、多样的解码器支撑结构而非单纯的参数减少；当参数量匹配时，稀疏与密集解码器间的部分性能差距来自编码器摊销。

Conclusion: Expander SAEs成功地在稀疏自编码器中实现了存储效率与性能保持的有效平衡，证明了结构化稀疏解码器设计的可行性。理论分析为该方法提供了可辨识性和恢复保证，为大规模神经网络激活分解提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose internal activations of neural networks into sparse linear combinations of learned features by fitting an overcomplete dictionary $\mathbf{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ with $m<n$, and inferring a sparse code $\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^n$ from $\mathbf{h}\approx\mathbf{W}\mathbf{x}$. This inference problem closely resembles the canonical setup of compressed sensing, but dense decoders requires $O(mn)$ learned values, which becomes costly at large feature counts. We introduce Expander SAEs: TopK SAEs whose decoder and tied encoder are supported on a left-$d$-regular expander mask with $d\ll m$, learning only $dn$ decoder values while keeping the sparse-coding problem $(m,n,k)$ fixed. The same structure reduces storage and turns the matching-pursuit correlation step $\mathbf{W}^\top \mathbf{r}$ in OMP into an $O(dn)$ gather-and-reduce operation. Our experiments show that across Pythia-70M/160M, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.2-1B residual-stream activations, varying $d$ traces a consistent storage--fidelity frontier, and that at the most compressed modern-LM setting, Qwen2.5-3B with $d=7$ uses $293\times$ fewer learned decoder values than the full dense decoder while retaining $84$% of dense CE-loss recovered. Control experiments show that the improved storage--fidelity tradeoff is driven by sparse, diverse decoder support structure rather than by fewer learned decoder values, and that when sparse and dense decoders are compared at matched parameter count, part of the remaining gap comes from encoder amortisation. On the theoretical side, we show that expansion and column flatness are sufficient for identifiability of noiseless $k$-sparse codes, and we derive complementary sufficient conditions under which OMP recovers the support exactly.

</details>


### [68] [Do LLMs Truly Generalize in the Molecular Domain? A Perturbation-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01800)
*Jiatong Li,Weida Wang,Changmeng Zheng,Shufei Zhang,Yatao Bian,Xiao-yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分子扰动框架来探测分子大语言模型的泛化能力，发现模型对结构变化极度敏感，通过上下文调优可以部分缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在分子发现领域展现出潜力，但其基于离散序列token的概率特性与化学空间的严格拓扑约束之间存在差距。研究者希望探究分子LLMs是否能够超越其序列表示所诱导的局部邻域进行泛化，系统性地研究模型在结构扰动下的表现。

Method: 提出分子扰动框架（Molecular Perturbation framework），在受控的图编辑距离（Graph Edit Distance, GED）下生成训练分子的语法有效结构变体，用于探测分子LLMs的流形规律性。同时采用上下文调优（In-Context Tuning, ICT）方法，通过锚定在结构相似分子上的预测来缓解模型的脆弱性。

Result: 分析表明，即使是单个编辑操作也会导致常见分子任务的性能大幅下降，揭示了模型存在狭窄的局部信任区域，对结构变化表现出脆弱的敏感性。实验显示上下文调优可以在受控结构扰动下提供鲁棒性，能够部分扩展局部信任区域。

Conclusion: 分子大语言模型对结构变化具有脆弱的敏感性和狭窄的局部信任区域。上下文调优作为一种自然的方法，可以部分缓解这种脆弱性，为稳定分子LLMs对抗结构变化提供了一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in molecular discovery, yet a gap remains between their probabilistic nature over discrete sequential tokens and the rigid topological constraints of chemical space. This raises the question of whether molecular LLMs can generalize beyond the local neighborhoods induced by their sequence-based representations. To systematically investigate this question, we introduce a Molecular Perturbation framework that generates syntax-valid structural variants of training molecules under controlled Graph Edit Distance (GED) to probe the manifold regularity of molecular LLMs. Our analysis shows that even a single edit can cause substantial performance drops on common molecular tasks, revealing a narrow local trust region and fragile sensitivity to structural changes. Since similar molecules tend to exhibit similar properties, In-Context Tuning (ICT), which anchors predictions on structurally similar molecules, offers a natural way to mitigate such fragility. Our experiments also examine whether ICT confers robustness under controlled structural perturbations, and the results suggest that it can partially expand the local trust region and offer a promising direction for stabilizing molecular LLMs against structural variation.

</details>


### [69] [Gaming Consensus: Coordinated Manipulation in Crowdsourced Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01824)
*Nikil Roashan Selvam,Jay Baxter,Sophie Hilgard,Brad Miller,Keith Coleman,Ellen Vitercik,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了众包事实核查系统（如X和Meta使用的矩阵分解算法）的安全漏洞，发现协调用户可以通过策略性投票操纵共识机制，最多10.7%的低质量笔记可通过少于10次评分被提升至共识阈值以上。研究揭示了"无帮助"评分反直觉地提高帮助性分数的现象，并已在X的Community Notes算法中部署缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流社交媒体平台（X、Meta、TikTok、Google）广泛采用众包事实核查系统，使用桥接机制识别误导信息，但缺乏对核心矩阵分解算法安全性的公开研究。现有系统仅依赖简单的支持者多样性而非多数支持，存在被协调用户利用潜在表示制造虚假共识的风险，亟需评估其脆弱性。

Method: 1) 基于历史生产数据对X和Meta的矩阵分解桥接算法进行实证分析；
2) 构建理论框架分析协调用户如何利用潜在表示进行策略性投票；
3) 开发操纵成本模型量化攻击所需努力；
4) 设计并部署针对虚假共识的缓解措施到X的Community Notes算法中。

Result: 1) 实证发现：最多10.7%的低质量笔记可通过少于10次评分被操纵超过共识阈值；
2) 理论发现：反直觉地，"无帮助"评分可以提高笔记的帮助性分数；
3) 量化了操纵努力的成本模型，揭示了系统的脆弱性程度。

Conclusion: 当前基于矩阵分解的众包事实核查桥接机制存在显著安全漏洞，协调用户能够以低成本制造虚假共识。研究提出的缓解措施已部署到X平台，为未来设计更鲁棒的事实核查系统提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Crowdsourced fact-checking systems have been adopted by major social media companies such as X, Meta, TikTok and Google with the aim of combating misleading information at scale without relying on centralized editorial control. These systems have been developed around a common underlying concept: a bridging mechanism that identifies notes flagging misleading information when they receive support from people with different perspectives rather than simple majority support. To our knowledge the only publicly disclosed bridging algorithms deployed for fact-checking are based on matrix factorization, as deployed by both X and Meta, augmented with additional components addressing abuse, targeted manipulation, and contributor brigades. This work examines the core matrix factorization portion of these systems, presenting theoretical and empirical evaluations of the degree to which coordinated users could vote strategically by leveraging the latent representations to fabricate the appearance of synthetic consensus within the bridging mechanism. Using historic production data, we find that up to 10.7% of lower quality notes could be manipulated above consensus thresholds using less than 10 ratings. We complement these findings with a theoretical analysis, revealing counterintuitively that rating a note as "Not Helpful" can increase its helpfulness score, as well as a cost model quantifying manipulation effort. We have developed and deployed mitigations within X's Community Notes algorithm to address synthetic consensus.

</details>


### [70] [Many Voices, One Reward: Multi-Role Rubric Generation for LLM Judging and Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01830)
*Dazhi Fu,Jiuding Yang,Yiwen Guo,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多角色评分标准生成框架(MRRG)，通过多角色互补消除评估维度盲区，在偏好验证和强化学习中均优于单角色基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无标注评分标准生成器通常依赖单一通用评估者，可能导致维度盲点问题，即忽视人类偏好的重要维度。作者旨在解决这一局限性，提升大语言模型在开放性任务中评估和优化的可靠性。

Method: 提出多角色评分标准生成(MRRG)框架，这是一个免训练、免参考的方法。该框架从多个互补角色中提取评估标准，并将其整合为可审计的基于评分标准的评分器。该评分器既可用于验证成对偏好，也可为GRPO风格的可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)提供奖励信号。

Result: 在偏好验证基准测试中，MRRG在多个骨干模型上均一致优于单角色评分标准生成基线。在RLVR实验中，MRRG为改进开放性生成提供了更强的奖励信号。

Conclusion: MRRG框架通过多角色互补有效解决了维度盲点问题，在偏好验证和强化学习优化任务中均展现出优越性能，为大语言模型的评估与优化提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable reward and preference signals are critical for evaluating and optimizing large language models on open-ended tasks. Rubric-based judges offer a transparent way to decompose such judgments into explicit evaluation criteria, but existing annotation-free rubric generators typically rely on a single generic evaluator. As a result, they may overlook important dimensions of human preference, a failure mode we term dimensional blind spots.
