### [211] [Cache Merging as a Convergent Replicated State for Multi-Agent Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01308)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了CanonicalMerge方法，通过基于内容的规范排序解决了多智能体潜在推理中KV-cache合并的非交换性问题，实现了字节级等价的确定性合并，性能匹配最佳BagMerge排序且无需预知最佳顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法BagMerge通过RoPE重编码沿序列轴拼接缓存，具有非交换性，最佳输入顺序不可预测且随机制、预算和模型规模变化。缺乏结构保证导致性能不稳定。

Method: 提出CanonicalMerge：1) 按中间层的平均K-norm对缓存进行内容排序，使合并缓存在任意输入排列下字节等价；2) 将复制状态与解码时布局分离，状态为内容寻址的潜在片段集合，合并操作为集合并集（基于状态的CvRDT，满足交换、结合、幂等、吸收性），CanonicalMerge作为其确定性渲染。

Result: 在分区推理基准测试中，CanonicalMerge在每个机制-预算-排序单元中均匹配最佳BagMerge排序性能（无需预知最佳顺序），精度损失小且统计不显著；重复输入被吸收而非重复拼接；在HotpotQA上成功迁移；相比训练无关的输出融合基线PackLLM在相同预算下领先45分。

Conclusion: CanonicalMerge为多智能体潜在推理提供了无条件的结构保证（交换性、确定性），将缓存级合并与输出级融合区分开来；k>2时可传输并共置潜在痕迹但尚未自动组合，为未来工作指明方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent latent reasoning composes agents' KV-caches into one context for a final agent. Prior work (Agent Primitives) does this by concatenating caches along the sequence axis with RoPE re-encoding, which we call BagMerge. BagMerge is non-commutative, and the best input ordering is unpredictable, shifting with the regime, the latent-step budget, and the model scale. We make this exchange a convergent replicated state. First, CanonicalMerge fixes the layout by content: ordering caches by mean K-norm at a middle layer renders the merged cache byte-identical under any input permutation, verified algorithmically (arity N<=5) and bit-for-bit on real Qwen3-1.7B and 4B state. Second, we separate the replicated state from decode-time layout: the state is a set of content-addressed latent fragments whose merge is set union, a state-based CvRDT (commutative, associative, idempotent, absorbing), and CanonicalMerge is its deterministic render. Because the render is byte-equivalent, every N=2 accuracy number carries over unchanged and re-delivered duplicates are absorbed rather than re-concatenated. On a partitioned-reasoning benchmark, CanonicalMerge matches the best BagMerge ordering in every regime-by-budget-by-ordering cell without knowing which order is best, trading a small, statistically insignificant accuracy margin for an unconditional structural guarantee. The behaviour transfers to real multi-document QA (HotpotQA), while the closest training-free output-fusion baseline (PackLLM) loses by 45 points at matched budget, placing cache-level merging in a regime distinct from output-level fusion. Finally, at k>2 the approach transports and colocates latent traces but does not by itself compose them, which we characterize to motivate future work.

</details>


### [212] [Simulation Based Reward Function Validation for Multi-Agent On Orbit Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01367)
*Patrick Quinn,Bala Prenith Reddy Gopu,George M. Nehma,Madhur Tiwari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维重建分析信息的通用奖励函数，用于多智能体强化学习（MARL）控制的航天器在轨检测任务，使智能体能够自主决策图像采集时机和位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL在轨检测任务的奖励函数主要关注于到达目标周围有限的预定检测点，缺乏通用性。本研究旨在开发一种通用的奖励函数，以评估任意位置和任意数量的图像，从而提升检测任务的灵活性和效果。

Method: 基于对在轨检测物体的三维重建分析，设计并开发了一种通用的奖励函数。该奖励函数允许训练后的智能体对图像采集时机拥有完全控制权，并可在任意位置评估任意数量的图像。

Result: 通过所提出的通用奖励函数，智能体能够自主决定图像采集的时机和位置。研究不仅为特定的MARL检测任务提供了最佳实践的见解，还为更广泛的检测任务提供了关键的经验总结。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的通用奖励函数方法有效提升了MARL在轨检测任务的灵活性和自主性，为航天器群体检测控制及相关领域的研究提供了有价值的参考和实践指导。

Abstract: A proposed method for the control of groups of inspection spacecraft is Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). While MARL has already been employed for this purpose in previous work, the reward functions used focus on reaching a finite set of predetermined inspection points around the target. In this work, we study and develop a generalized reward function for the MARL inspection task informed by the analysis of 3D reconstructions of inspected objects in orbit. Because the reward function is generalized such that any number of images at arbitrary locations may evaluated, we also allow trained agents to have complete control over when images are collected. With this approach, we gather insights into best practices for not only the specific MARL inspection task, but also gain key takeaways informative to the broader inspection task outside of a MARL context.

</details>


### [213] [Congestion-Based Slot Pricing in a Railway Auction Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01822)
*Bill Roungas,Sebastiaan Meijer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了放松管制下的铁路时刻分配问题，设计了一个基于拍卖的多智能体系统，通过拥堵定价和非对称纠正机制来分配稀缺资源，发现纠正机制虽能触发激励但不足以完全抑制大型运营商的策略性主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于铁路行业放松管制背景下的时刻分配问题。在市场化改革中，稀缺的铁路时刻资源需要在异质的、具有策略行为的多个运营商之间进行分配。大型运营商可能利用规模优势进行策略性主导，而现有机制难以在保持透明度和拥堵敏感性的同时有效抑制这种市场支配行为，因此需要设计新的分配机制。

Method: 设计了一个实时网络多智能体环境，采用重复博弈框架建模智能体交互。核心机制包含两部分：(1)基于拥堵的基础价格，随总需求增加而上升；(2)非对称纠正调整，惩罚请求最多时刻的智能体并奖励请求最少的智能体。系统邀请领域专家作为参与者控制各运营商智能体，在有限时间内进行多轮拍卖决策，并可观察边际成本和竞争对手反馈。进行了两次结构化实验会议进行验证。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1)拥堵机制能按设计响应总需求变化，纠正激励被成功触发；(2)然而，代表大型运营商的智能体在面临惩罚时仍坚持高请求策略，表明纠正定价机制虽必要但不足以完全中和策略性主导；(3)事后访谈表明参与者的决策由角色设定而非个人倾向驱动，除短期利润最大化外，还存在保持市场存在感、提高竞争对手成本等策略动机。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在非对称预算条件下设计多智能体机制的复杂性：单纯的纠正性定价无法完全抑制大型运营商的策略主导，需要更综合的机制设计。研究为理解放松管制环境下运营商的策略行为提供了实证基础，并为后续分析性验证和大规模多智能体实验指明了方向。研究强调机制设计需同时考虑透明性、拥堵敏感性和对策略性主导的有效抑制。

Abstract: We present a multi-agent system for studying the allocation of discrete, congested resources among heterogeneous strategic agents, motivated by the problem of railway slot allocation under deregulation. Multiple operator-agents, differing in size and capacity, interact through a shared auction mechanism over repeated rounds under time-constrained decision-making. The mechanism combines a congestion-based base price that increases with aggregate demand with an asymmetric corrective adjustment that penalises the agent requesting the most slots and rewards the agent requesting the fewest, and is designed to mitigate strategic dominance by large agents while preserving transparency and congestion sensitivity. We formulate the interaction as a repeated game with incomplete information and implement the system as a real-time, web-based multi-agent environment in which human participants control individual agents and observe live marginal-cost and competitor feedback.
  We report exploratory observations from two structured sessions with domain experts acting as operator-agents. The congestion mechanism responds to aggregate demand as designed and the corrective incentives are actively triggered, but agents representing large operators persist with high-request strategies despite the penalty, suggesting that corrective pricing is necessary but not sufficient to neutralise strategic dominance in this multi-agent setting. A post-session debrief indicates that participants' decisions were driven by the assumed agent role rather than personal disposition, and provides qualitative support for strategic motives, such as preserving market presence and raising rivals' costs, operating alongside short-term profit maximisation. We discuss implications for multi-agent mechanism design under asymmetric budgets and outline directions for analytical validation and larger-scale multi-agent experiments.

</details>


### [214] [CausalSteward: An Agentic Divide-Conquer-Combine Copilot for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01936)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Gian Lorenzo Marchioni,Moritz Willig,Juergen Luettin,Lavdim Halilaj,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了CAST框架，一种人机协作的多智能体系统，通过分治策略融合先验知识与数据驱动方法，解决高维因果发现中的可识别性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据因果学习面临严峻挑战，核心假设的违反导致因果可识别性问题。虽然大量先验知识蕴含宝贵的因果信息，但如何有效将其整合到因果发现过程中仍是一个未解决的难题。

Method: 采用人机协作的多智能体协同系统，基于分治策略迭代划分大规模变量簇并进行独立分析。融合检索增强生成(RAG)和条件独立性检验等定制工具，实现先验知识与数据驱动方法的结合。

Result: 成功开发了CAST框架，能够在交互式环境中组装大规模因果模型，验证了多智能体框架在因果推理中的能力与局限性，以及人在回路对提高结果准确性和可信度的贡献。

Conclusion: 多智能体因果推理框架结合人在回路机制，能够有效处理高维因果发现问题，为构建准确可信的因果模型提供了新的解决方案，同时揭示了该方法的能力边界。

Abstract: Learning causal models from high-dimensional data is a significant challenge, particularly in real-world settings where violations of core assumptions lead to causal identifiability issues. Although massive amounts of prior knowledge are available, and contain valuable causal information, effectively integrating this knowledge into the causal discovery process remains an open problem. We introduce CausalSTeward (CAST), a novel human-in-the-loop framework for interactively assembling large causal models. CausalSteward is a multi-agent collaborative system that tackles high-dimensional causality through a divide-and-conquer approach where large clusters of variables are iteratively partitioned and then separately analyzed. Our framework fuses prior knowledge with a data-driven approach by using tailored tools such as retrieval augmented generation and conditional independence tests. Finally, we use this work to examine the capabilities and limitations of causal reasoning in multi-agent frameworks, and how the human-in-the-loop can contribute to accurate and trustworthy results.

</details>


### [215] [AgentsCAD: Automated Design for Manufacturing of FDM Parts via Multi-Agent LLM Reasoning and Geometric Feature Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02448)
*Emmanuel George,Christopher Keefe,Peter Pak,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgentsCAD，一个多智能体系统，通过结合B-Rep几何与LLM推理实现DFAM自动化，能够检测悬垂缺陷并自动生成修改建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有切片软件能识别如陡峭悬垂等缺陷，但无法修改底层几何模型；需要一种方法将原始B-Rep几何与LLM推理相结合，以自动化目标化的制造设计修改。

Method: 1. 解析STEP文件，检测超过45°阈值的悬垂面；2. 构建面邻接拓扑图，可选注入GraphSAGE模型训练的语义特征标签；3. 使用Claude Sonnet智能体推荐重定向、圆角、倒角等修改；4. 用GPT-4o视觉语言验证器检查渲染视图确认几何完整性；5. 输出修改后的STEP文件和可读报告。

Result: 在鸟屋模型测试案例中，系统正确诊断了悬垂问题，选择了合适的缺陷缓解策略，并提出了物理上有效的修正方案，部分解决了LLM驱动的CAD修改中的几何到语言转换问题。

Conclusion: AgentsCAD成功将几何B-Rep与LLM推理相结合，实现了自动化DFAM流程，能够识别几何缺陷并生成有效修改建议，为LLM驱动的CAD修改提供了可行的解决方案框架。

Abstract: Parts manufactured with Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) often require Design for Additive Manufacturing (DFAM) modifications to ensure printability, structural integrity, and reduced post-processing. Current slicers identify defects such as steep overhangs but are unable to modify the underlying geometry. This work presents AgentsCAD, a multi-agent system that bridges raw boundary-representation (B-Rep) geometry and Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning to automate targeted DFM. The workflow begins by parsing a STEP file. The agentic system detects overhangs above a 45°threshold, constructs a face-adjacency topology graph, and optionally injects semantic feature labels from a GraphSAGE model trained on MFCAD++ (59,665 parts), before dispatching a Claude Sonnet design-reasoning agent that recommends reorientations, fillets, chamfers, and similar modifications. A GPT-4o vision-language verifier inspects rendered views to confirm geometric integrity. Outputs include a modified STEP file and a human-readable report. A test case on a birdhouse model demonstrates that the system correctly diagnoses overhangs, selects appropriate defect mitigation strategies, and proposes physically valid corrections, partially solving the geometry-to-language translation problem central to LLM-driven CAD modification.

</details>


### [216] [Adoption and Ecosystem Health: A Longitudinal Analysis of Open-Source Multi-Agent Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02453)
*Xi Zhang,Papi Menon,Vivian Chu,Koray Cosguner*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: GitHub stars不能可靠衡量开源AI Agent框架的健康度；应采用贡献者密度、跨生态系统参与度和留存率等指标进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2022年11月ChatGPT发布以来，开源AI Agent框架快速涌现，工程团队在选择框架时往往依赖GitHub stars等流行度信号，但这些信号可能掩盖了框架生态的真实健康状况，因此需要更科学的评估方法。

Method: 收集2022年末至2026年初15个主要开源AI Agent框架仓库的数据，包括808,042个star、73,997个pull request、86,241次commit和987,330个用户画像，从认知度、采用度和留存率三个维度分析生态系统健康状况，提出贡献者密度（每1000个star对应的贡献者数量）等指标。

Result: 发现三点：1) headline流行度不可靠，stars反映炒作周期和非有机活动，如AutoGPT单月获111,967个star但贡献者密度低于LangChain；2) 可见性与参与度背离，LangChain作为共享基础设施吸引了82.5%的跨生态贡献者；3) 留存率在前30天下降最陡峭，90天后趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 生态系统健康程度应通过贡献者密度、跨生态系统参与度和留存率来衡量，而非仅看stars，这些指标为工程团队提供了更稳健的框架评估基础。

Abstract: Since ChatGPT's launch in November 2022, open-source agentic frameworks have proliferated, making framework selection important for engineering teams while obscured by popularity signals such as GitHub stars. This paper analyzes 15 major open-source AI agent framework repositories from late 2022 to early 2026, using 808,042 stars, 73,997 pull requests, 86,241 commits, and 987,330 user profiles to assess ecosystem health across awareness, adoption, and retention. Three findings emerge. First, headline popularity is unreliable. Star counts reflect hype cycles and inorganic activity. AutoGPT gained 111,967 stars in one month but converted fewer than 9 contributors per 1,000 stars, defined as contributor density in this research, compared with LangChain's 41. Lower-profile frameworks such as Pydantic-AI show higher contributor density, indicating deeper adoption. Second, mapping awareness against adoption shows that visibility and engagement diverge. MetaGPT and LangFlow have contributor density ratios below 5 even with their high visibility. Openai-agents-python's limited contributor base suggests institutional backing alone does not ensure community depth. By analyzing cross-framework contribution, we discover that LangChain functions as a shared infrastructure, attracting 82.5% of cross-ecosystem contributors. Third, retention drops most steeply in the first 30 days of initial contribution and stabilizes near 90 days. Overall, ecosystem health is better measured by contributor density, cross-ecosystem engagement, and retention than by stars alone. These metrics offer teams a more robust basis for framework evaluation.

</details>
